## PRE-FLIGHT (execute ALL in parallel before reading anything)

```bash
git log --oneline -7                        # recent history + commit style
git status --short                          # uncommitted changes
git diff HEAD --stat                        # change scope
npx tsc --noEmit 2>&1 | tail -20           # TypeScript strict check
docker ps --filter "name=mcorch" --format "{{.Names}}: {{.Status}}"
docker ps --filter "name=mega-brain" --format "{{.Names}}: {{.Status}}"
curl -s http://localhost:8001/api/v2/heartbeat  # Chroma API v2 health
ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null                    # BoK suites disponíveis
wc -l HANDOFF.md                            # total lines — drives the read-from-end offset
```

Read in parallel (HANDOFF.md uses **read-from-end strategy** — SSP-01 v6.5.0; arquivo monolítico newest-first em ~3170+ linhas, leitura completa estoura limite de 25k tokens):
- `HANDOFF.md` with `limit=71` → Task State header + FIRST ACTION FOR NEXT AGENT (sempre estável, contém summary da fase atual selada)
- `HANDOFF.md` with `offset=<total_lines - 400>` `limit=400` → últimas 1-2 Records + Pending Actions + GraphRAG State + Infrastructure + Key Files (suffix sections always at the tail)
- `CLAUDE.md` (architecture rules, data flow, key files — incluindo MCORCH Master Execution Protocol + Survival Laws link)
- `.claude/context/sprint-priorities.md` (sprint goal, 4Cs snapshot, top gaps)
- `/home/ubuntu/.claude/projects/-home-gcrUX-htdocs-constellation-orchestra/memory/MEMORY.md` (memory index)

> ⚠️ **Auditoria histórica de seals antigos:** usar `Read` com offset arbitrário em HANDOFF.md (fora do fluxo padrão de pickup). Ordering newest-first: seal mais antigo ≈ linha 2805, seal mais recente logo após linha 71. Cada `## <Phase> Record (YYYY-MM-DD)` marca um seal. Manter o Read em chunks ≤ 400 linhas para preservar budget de tokens.

Also check for loose files in scratch/:
```bash
ls scratch/ 2>/dev/null && echo "⚠️ scratch/ has files — consider moving to .claude/scripts/db/" || echo "scratch/ clean ✅"
```

### BoK Gate check (v3 — MCORCH Master Execution Protocol)

For each BoK suite in `docs/bok/<slug>/`, verify completeness:
```bash
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete" || echo "⚠️ $slug — missing: ${missing[*]}"
done
```

If the user mentions working on a **new module / complex feature / integration / architectural change**:
- Check whether `docs/bok/<expected-slug>/` exists with all 9 docs.
- If missing: surface in the **ALERTAS** section as a critical block per CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol.
- Recommend `/bok-scribe <idea summary>` before any code is written.

## Rules

- **TypeScript errors → report immediately.** If there are TS errors, list them under ALERTAS. Do not start implementing new features until errors are resolved (unless the user explicitly asks to proceed despite errors).
- **Unhealthy containers → report with triage.** Suggest the fix command if known (e.g., `docker compose restart mcorch-vector-engine` for chroma).
- **Uncommitted changes → describe, do not commit.** Only describe what exists in the working tree; committing is the user's call.
- **Keep the brief tight.** No padding. No repetition of CLAUDE.md architecture. The user already knows the architecture — they need the delta: what changed, what's broken, what's next.
- **Pending Actions priority order:** CRITICAL (before first client) > NON-CRITICAL > Phase Zeta > Core v3 > Epsilon v9.
- **Knowledge Mesh numbers:** use `GraphRAG State` section from HANDOFF.md as the source. Do not invent counts.

> 3308	Sessão de elevação da `ConstellationMatrix` ao nível de **Code Intelligence** e estabilização do motor gráfico. > 20	  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
> 21	  'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'POST, OPTIONS',

> 24	const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!;

> 28	interface Filter {
> 29	  column: string;
> 30	  op: FilterOp;
> 31	  value: unknown;
> 32	}
> 33	
> 34	interface OrderClause {
> 35	  column: string;
> 36	  ascending?: boolean;
> 37	}
> 38	
> 39	interface BridgeRequest {
> 40	  table: AllowedTable;
> 41	  select?: string;
> 42	  filters?: Filter[];
> 43	  order?: OrderClause;
> 44	  limit?: number;
> 45	  offset?: number;
> 46	}
> 47	
> 48	interface BridgeResponse {
> 49	  rows: unknown[];
> 50	  rowCount: number;
> 51	  table: string;
> 52	  queryHash: string;
> 53	  executedAt: string;
> 54	  durationMs: number;
> 55	}
> 56	
> 57	function json(body: unknown, status = 200) {
> 58	  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
> 59	    status,
> 60	    headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
> 61	  });
> 62	}
> 63	
> 64	function isForbidden(column: string): boolean {
> 65	  return FORBIDDEN_COLUMN_PATTERNS.some((re) => re.test(column));
> 66	}
> 67	
> 68	function validateSelectClause(select: string, allowed: readonly string[]): string | null {
> 69	  if (select.trim() === '*') return null;
> 70	  const cols = select
> 71	    .split(',')
> 72	    .map((c) => c.trim())
> 73	    .filter(Boolean);
> 74	  for (const c of cols) {
> 75	    if (isForbidden(c)) return `Forbidden column in select: ${c}`;
> 76	    if (!allowed.includes(c)) return `Column not whitelisted for this table: ${c}`;
> 77	  }
> 78	  return null;
> 79	}
> 80	
> 81	function validateFilters(
> 82	  filters: Filter[] | undefined,
> 83	  allowed: readonly string[],
> 84	): string | null {
> 85	  if (!filters) return null;
> 86	  if (!Array.isArray(filters)) return 'filters must be an array';
> 87	  for (const f of filters) {
> 88	    if (!f || typeof f !== 'object') return 'each filter must be an object';
> 89	    if (typeof f.column !== 'string') return 'filter.column must be a string';
> 90	    if (typeof f.op !== 'string') return 'filter.op must be a string';
> 91	    if (isForbidden(f.column)) return `Forbidden column in filter: ${f.column}`;
> 92	    if (!allowed.includes(f.column)) return `Column not whitelisted in filter: ${f.column}`;
> 93	    if (!ALLOWED_OPS.includes(f.op as FilterOp)) return `Operator not allowed: ${f.op}`;
> 94	  }
> 95	  return null;
> 96	}
> 97	
> 98	async function sha256Hex(input: string): Promise<string> {
> 99	  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(input);
> 100	  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', bytes);
> 101	  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest))
> 102	    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
> 103	    .join('');
> 104	}
> 105	
> 106	async function checkAdmin(userId: string): Promise<boolean> {
> 107	  const { data, error } = await admin.rpc('has_role', {
> 108	    _user_id: userId,
> 109	    _role: 'admin',
> 110	  });
> 111	  if (error) {
> 112	    console.error('[aios-sql-bridge] has_role error:', error);
> 113	    return false;
> 114	  }
> 115	  return data === true;
> 116	}

> 118	async function checkQuota(userId: string): Promise<{ ok: boolean; used: number }> {
> 119	  const startOfDay = new Date();
> 120	  startOfDay.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
> 121	  const { count, error } = await admin
> 122	    .from('usage_tracking')
> 123	    .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
> 124	    .eq('user_id', userId)
> 125	    .eq('resource_type', 'sql_bridge_query')
> 126	    .gte('created_at', startOfDay.toISOString());
> 127	  if (error) {
> 128	    console.error('[aios-sql-bridge] quota check error:', error);
> 129	    return { ok: true, used: 0 };
> 130	  }
> 131	  const used = count ?? 0;
> 132	  return { ok: used < DAILY_QUOTA, used };
> 133	}
> 134	
> 135	async function auditLog(params: {
> 136	  userId: string;
> 137	  table: string;
> 138	  queryHash: string;
> 139	  rowCount: number;
> 140	  durationMs: number;
> 141	  ok: boolean;
> 142	  error?: string;
> 143	}) {
> 144	  // best-effort — never block the response
> 145	  try {
> 146	    await admin.from('usage_tracking').insert({
> 147	      user_id: params.userId,
> 148	      resource_type: 'sql_bridge_query',
> 149	      quantity: 1,
> 150	      credits_consumed: 0,
> 151	      metadata: {
> 152	        table: params.table,
> 153	        queryHash: params.queryHash,
> 154	        rowCount: params.rowCount,
> 155	        durationMs: params.durationMs,
> 156	        ok: params.ok,
> 157	        error: params.error ?? null,
> 158	      },
> 159	    });
> 160	  } catch (e) {
> 161	    console.error('[aios-sql-bridge] usage_tracking insert failed:', e);
> 162	  }
> 163	  try {
> 164	    await admin.from('infra_health_logs').insert({
> 165	      service: 'aios-sql-bridge',
> 166	      status: params.ok ? 'healthy' : 'unhealthy',
> 167	    });
> 168	  } catch (e) {
> 169	    console.error('[aios-sql-bridge] infra_health_logs insert failed:', e);
> 170	  }
> 171	}
> 172	
> 173	Deno.serve(async (req) => {
> 174	  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
> 175	    return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
> 176	  }
> 177	  if (req.method !== 'POST') {
> 178	    return json({ error: 'Method not allowed' }, 405);
> 179	  }
> 180	
> 181	  const startedAt = performance.now();
> 182	  let userId = 'anonymous';
> 183	  let body: BridgeRequest | null = null;
> 184	  let queryHash = '';
> 185	
> 186	  try {
> 187	    const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization');
> 188	    if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
> 189	      return json({ error: 'Missing or malformed Authorization header' }, 401);
> 190	    }
> 191	    const token = authHeader.slice('Bearer '.length);
> 192	    const { data: userData, error: authError } = await admin.auth.getUser(token);
> 193	    if (authError || !userData?.user) {
> 194	      return json({ error: 'Invalid JWT' }, 401);
> 195	    }
> 196	    userId = userData.user.id;
> 197	
> 198	    const isAdmin = await checkAdmin(userId);
> 199	    if (!isAdmin) {
> 200	      return json({ error: 'Admin role required for aios-sql-bridge (SSP-01 v1)' }, 403);
> 201	    }
> 202	
> 203	    const quota = await checkQuota(userId);
> 204	    if (!quota.ok) {
> 205	      return json(
> 206	        {
> 207	          error: 'Daily quota exceeded',
> 208	          limit: DAILY_QUOTA,
> 209	          used: quota.used,
> 210	        },
> 211	        429,
> 212	      );
> 213	    }
> 214	
> 215	    try {
> 216	      body = (await req.json()) as BridgeRequest;
> 217	    } catch {
> 218	      return json({ error: 'Body must be valid JSON' }, 400);
> 219	    }
> 220	
> 221	    if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || typeof body.table !== 'string') {
> 222	      return json({ error: 'Body must include a "table" string field' }, 400);
> 223	    }
> 224	    if (!(body.table in WHITELIST)) {
> 225	      return json(
> 226	        {
> 227	          error: 'Table not whitelisted',
> 228	          allowed: Object.keys(WHITELIST),
> 229	        },
> 230	        400,
> 231	      );
> 232	    }
> 233	
> 234	    const spec = WHITELIST[body.table];
> 235	    const select = (body.select && body.select.trim()) || spec.defaultSelect;
> 236	
> 237	    const selectErr = validateSelectClause(select, spec.columns);
> 238	    if (selectErr) return json({ error: selectErr }, 400);
> 239	
> 240	    const filterErr = validateFilters(body.filters, spec.columns);
> 241	    if (filterErr) return json({ error: filterErr }, 400);
> 242	
> 243	    if (body.order) {
> 244	      if (typeof body.order.column !== 'string') {
> 245	        return json({ error: 'order.column must be a string' }, 400);
> 246	      }
> 247	      if (isForbidden(body.order.column) || !spec.columns.includes(body.order.column)) {
> 248	        return json({ error: `Order column not whitelisted: ${body.order.column}` }, 400);
> 249	      }
> 250	    }
> 251	
> 252	    const limit = Math.min(Math.max(body.limit ?? DEFAULT_LIMIT, 1), MAX_LIMIT);
> 253	    const offset = Math.min(Math.max(body.offset ?? 0, 0), MAX_OFFSET);
> 254	
> 255	    queryHash = await sha256Hex(
> 256	      JSON.stringify({
> 257	        table: body.table,
> 258	        select,
> 259	        filters: body.filters ?? [],
> 260	        order: body.order ?? null,
> 261	        limit,
> 262	        offset,
> 263	      }),
> 264	    );
> 265	
> 266	    let q = admin.from(body.table).select(select, { count: 'exact' });
> 267	    for (const f of body.filters ?? []) {
> 268	      const op = f.op as FilterOp;
> 269	      if (op === 'in') {
> 270	        const arr = Array.isArray(f.value) ? (f.value as unknown[]) : [];
> 271	        q = q.in(f.column, arr as never[]);
> 272	      } else if (op === 'is') {
> 273	        q = q.is(f.column, f.value as never);
> 274	      } else {
> 275	        q = (q as unknown as Record<string, (c: string, v: unknown) => typeof q>)[op](
> 276	          f.column,
> 277	          f.value,
> 278	        );
> 279	      }
> 280	    }
> 281	    if (body.order) {
> 282	      q = q.order(body.order.column, { ascending: body.order.ascending ?? true });
> 283	    }
> 284	    q = q.range(offset, offset + limit - 1);
> 285	
> 286	    const { data, error, count } = await q;
> 287	    const durationMs = Math.round(performance.now() - startedAt);
> 288	
> 289	    if (error) {
> 290	      await auditLog({
> 291	        userId,
> 292	        table: body.table,
> 293	        queryHash,
> 294	        rowCount: 0,
> 295	        durationMs,
> 296	        ok: false,
> 297	        error: error.message,
> 298	      });
> 299	      return json({ error: error.message, queryHash }, 500);
> 300	    }
> 301	
> 302	    const rows = data ?? [];
> 303	    await auditLog({
> 304	      userId,
> 305	      table: body.table,
> 306	      queryHash,
> 307	      rowCount: rows.length,
> 308	      durationMs,
> 309	      ok: true,
> 310	    });
> 311	
> 312	    const response: BridgeResponse = {
> 313	      rows,
> 314	      rowCount: count ?? rows.length,
> 315	      table: body.table,
> 316	      queryHash,
> 317	      executedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
> 318	      durationMs,
> 319	    };
> 320	    return json(response, 200);
> 321	  } catch (e) {
> 322	    const durationMs = Math.round(performance.now() - startedAt);
> 323	    const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Unknown error';
> 324	    console.error('[aios-sql-bridge] fatal:', message);
> 325	    await auditLog({
> 326	      userId,
> 327	      table: body?.table ?? 'unknown',
> 328	      queryHash,
> 329	      rowCount: 0,
> 330	      durationMs,
> 331	      ok: false,
> 332	      error: message,
> 333	    });
> 334	    return json({ error: message }, 500);
> 335	  }
> 336	});
> 337	
> ```

> 298	async function generateOpenAI(apiKey: string, body: ExecuteRequest) {
> 299	  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("OpenAI API key not configured");
> 300	
> 301	  const model = body.model || "dall-e-3";
> 302	  const size = body.parameters?.size || "1024x1024";
> 303	
> 304	  const resp = await fetch("https://api.openai.com/v1/images/generations", {
> 305	    method: "POST",
> 306	    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
> 307	    body: JSON.stringify({
> 308	      model,
> 309	      prompt: body.prompt,
> 310	      n: 1,
> 311	      size,
> 312	      response_format: "b64_json",
> 313	      ...(body.parameters?.quality ? { quality: body.parameters.quality } : {}),
> 314	      ...(body.parameters?.style ? { style: body.parameters.style } : {}),
> 315	    }),
> 316	  });
> 317	
> 318	  if (!resp.ok) {
> 319	    const t = await resp.text();
> 320	    throw new Error(`OpenAI error ${resp.status}: ${t}`);
> 321	  }
> 322	
> 323	  const data = await resp.json();
> 324	  return { url: data.data?.[0]?.url || "", b64: data.data?.[0]?.b64_json || "" };
> 325	}

> 327	async function generateReplicate(apiKey: string, body: ExecuteRequest) {
> 328	  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("Replicate API key not configured");
> 329	
> 330	  const modelMap: Record<string, string> = {
> 331	    "flux-1.1-pro": "black-forest-labs/flux-1.1-pro",
> 332	    "flux-schnell": "black-forest-labs/flux-schnell",
> 333	    "sdxl": "stability-ai/sdxl:39ed52f2a78e934b3ba6e2a89f5b1c712de7dfea535525255b1aa35c5565e08b",
> 334	  };
> 335	
> 336	  const modelId = modelMap[body.model] || body.model;
> 337	  const input: Record<string, any> = {
> 338	    prompt: body.prompt,
> 339	    ...(body.negative_prompt ? { negative_prompt: body.negative_prompt } : {}),
> 340	    ...(body.parameters?.width ? { width: body.parameters.width } : {}),
> 341	    ...(body.parameters?.height ? { height: body.parameters.height } : {}),
> 342	    ...(body.parameters?.num_inference_steps ? { num_inference_steps: body.parameters.num_inference_steps } : {}),
> 343	    ...(body.parameters?.guidance_scale ? { guidance_scale: body.parameters.guidance_scale } : {}),
> 344	  };
> 345	
> 346	  // Create prediction
> 347	  const createResp = await fetch("https://api.replicate.com/v1/predictions", {
> 348	    method: "POST",
> 349	    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
> 350	    body: JSON.stringify({ model: modelId.includes(":") ? undefined : modelId, version: modelId.includes(":") ? modelId.split(":")[1] : undefined, input }),
> 351	  });
> 352	
> 353	  if (!createResp.ok) {
> 354	    const t = await createResp.text();
> 355	    throw new Error(`Replicate error ${createResp.status}: ${t}`);
> 356	  }
> 357	
> 358	  let prediction = await createResp.json();
> 359	
> 360	  // Poll for completion (max 120s)
> 361	  const maxWait = 120_000;
> 362	  const start = Date.now();
> 363	  while (prediction.status !== "succeeded" && prediction.status !== "failed" && Date.now() - start < maxWait) {
> 364	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2000));
> 365	    const pollResp = await fetch(prediction.urls.get, {
> 366	      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}` },
> 367	    });
> 368	    prediction = await pollResp.json();
> 369	  }
> 370	
> 371	  if (prediction.status === "failed") {
> 372	    throw new Error(`Replicate failed: ${prediction.error}`);
> 373	  }
> 374	
> 375	  const output = Array.isArray(prediction.output) ? prediction.output[0] : prediction.output;
> 376	  return { url: output || "" };
> 377	}
> 378

> 408	async function generateHiggsfield(apiKey: string, apiSecret: string, body: ExecuteRequest) {
> 409	  if (!apiKey || !apiSecret) {
> 410	    throw new Error("Higgsfield credentials not configured (HIGGSFIELD_API_KEY / HIGGSFIELD_API_KEY_SECRET)");
> 411	  }
> 412	
> 413	  // Correct Higgsfield Platform API — base + auth + routes per the tested
> 414	  // viralmind integration (src/lib/higgsfield.server.ts). The previous
> 415	  // api.higgsfield.ai/v1/generations endpoint was wrong and never worked.
> 416	  const BASE_URL = "https://platform.higgsfield.ai";
> 417	  const authHeader = `Key ${apiKey}:${apiSecret}`;
> 418	  const POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 3000;
> 419	  const MAX_POLL_MS = 90_000;
> 420	
> 421	  // Derive Soul aspect ratio from incoming size / aspect_ratio params.
> 422	  // Canonical Higgsfield ARs (per /docs/guides/images): 16:9 · 9:16 · 1:1 · 4:5.
> 423	  const SIZE_TO_AR: Record<string, string> = {
> 424	    "1024x1024": "1:1",
> 425	    "1792x1024": "16:9",
> 426	    "1024x1792": "9:16",
> 427	    "1024x1280": "4:5",
> 428	    "1280x1024": "5:4",
> 429	  };
> 430	  const VALID_AR = new Set(["16:9", "9:16", "1:1", "4:5"]);
> 431	  const sizeKey = body.parameters?.size ? String(body.parameters.size) : "";
> 432	  const requestedAr = body.parameters?.aspect_ratio || SIZE_TO_AR[sizeKey] || "1:1";
> 433	  const aspectRatio = VALID_AR.has(requestedAr) ? requestedAr : "1:1";
> 434	  const resolution = body.parameters?.resolution === "720p" ? "720p" : "1080p";
> 435	
> 436	  // NOTE (Phase 5.3 — TODO): video routes (DoP/Kling/Seedance) will be added
> 437	  // here using `?hf_webhook=URL` query param (NOT body) per validation doc
> 438	  // .claude/context/higgsfield-api-validation-2026-05-15.md § Webhook spec.
> 439	  // Video gens exceed the current 90s poll window — webhook is mandatory.
> 440	
> 441	  // ── Submit Soul text-to-image job ──────────────────────────────────
> 442	  const submitResp = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/higgsfield-ai/soul/standard`, {
> 443	    method: "POST",
> 444	    headers: {
> 445	      Authorization: authHeader,
> 446	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
> 447	      Accept: "application/json",
> 448	    },
> 449	    body: JSON.stringify({ prompt: body.prompt, aspect_ratio: aspectRatio, resolution }),
> 450	  });
> 451	
> 452	  if (!submitResp.ok) {
> 453	    const t = await submitResp.text();
> 454	    if (submitResp.status === 401 || submitResp.status === 403) {
> 455	      throw new Error("Higgsfield: credenciais inválidas");
> 456	    }
> 457	    if (submitResp.status === 402) {
> 458	      throw new Error("Higgsfield: saldo de créditos esgotado");
> 459	    }
> 460	    if (submitResp.status === 429) {
> 461	      throw new Error("Higgsfield: limite de requisições atingido");
> 462	    }
> 463	    throw new Error(`Higgsfield submit error ${submitResp.status}: ${t.slice(0, 200)}`);
> 464	  }
> 465	
> 466	  const submitData = await submitResp.json();
> 467	  const requestId = submitData.request_id || submitData.id;
> 468	  if (!requestId) throw new Error("Higgsfield: resposta de submit inválida (sem request_id)");
> 469	
> 470	  // ── Poll until terminal state ──────────────────────────────────────
> 471	  const started = Date.now();
> 472	  while (Date.now() - started < MAX_POLL_MS) {
> 473	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, POLL_INTERVAL_MS));
> 474	    const statusResp = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/requests/${requestId}/status`, {
> 475	      method: "GET",
> 476	      headers: { Authorization: authHeader, Accept: "application/json" },
> 477	    });
> 478	    if (!statusResp.ok) {
> 479	      const t = await statusResp.text();
> 480	      throw new Error(`Higgsfield status error ${statusResp.status}: ${t.slice(0, 200)}`);
> 481	    }
> 482	    const statusData = await statusResp.json();
> 483	    if (statusData.status === "completed") {
> 484	      const url = statusData.images?.[0]?.url || statusData.video?.url || "";
> 485	      if (!url) throw new Error("Higgsfield: geração concluída sem URL de saída");
> 486	      return { url };
> 487	    }
> 488	    if (statusData.status === "failed") throw new Error("Higgsfield: a geração falhou no provedor");
> 489	    if (statusData.status === "nsfw") throw new Error("Higgsfield: conteúdo bloqueado por moderação");
> 490	    if (statusData.status === "cancelled") throw new Error("Higgsfield: geração cancelada");
> 491	  }
> 492	  throw new Error("Higgsfield: geração demorou demais (timeout >90s)");
> 493	}
> 494	
> ```

> 265	
> 266	**Correção material à §2 Pillar 3:** leitura de `canvas-execute/index.ts:232-267` confirmou que o deduct está guarded por `if (!error)` — débito é atomic on success. O campo `vm_canvas_executions.credits_charged` é INSERTado upfront mas NÃO reflete dedução real em failures. Os 68 mcoCoins listados nos 5 failed executions **não** foram debitados do balance (apenas registrados como intenção). **Naming/UX bug confirmado**, **financial bug retirado**. > 17	CREATE INDEX idx_mcoin_transactions_action ON mcoin_transactions(action);
> 18	
> 19	-- 3. Enable RLS
> 20	ALTER TABLE mcoin_transactions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
> 21	
> 22	-- 4. RLS policy
> 23	CREATE POLICY "mcoin_users_own" ON mcoin_transactions 
> 24	FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
> 25	
> 26	-- 5. RPC function (award_mco_coins)
> 27	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION award_mco_coins(
> 28	  p_user_id UUID,
> 29	  p_amount INTEGER,
> 30	  p_action TEXT,
> 31	  p_context JSONB DEFAULT '{}'
> 32	) RETURNS VOID
> 33	LANGUAGE plpgsql
> 34	SECURITY DEFINER
> 35	AS '
> 36	BEGIN
> 37	  IF p_amount <= 0 THEN
> 38	    RAISE EXCEPTION ''Amount must be positive'';
> 39	  END IF;
> 40	  IF p_amount > 1000 THEN
> 41	    RAISE EXCEPTION ''Single award cannot exceed 1000 mcoCoins'';
> 42	  END IF;
> 43	  UPDATE profiles
> 44	  SET mco_balance = COALESCE(mco_balance, 0) + p_amount,
> 45	      updated_at = NOW()
> 46	  WHERE id = p_user_id;
> 47	  INSERT INTO mcoin_transactions (user_id, action, amount, context)
> 48	  VALUES (p_user_id, p_action, p_amount, p_context);
> 49	END;
> 50	';
> 51	
> 52	-- 6. Grant permissions
> 53	GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION award_mco_coins TO authenticated;
> 54	GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION award_mco_coins TO service_role;

> 51	      'created_at',
> 52	    ],
> 53	    defaultSelect:
> 54	      'id, user_id, provider, model, status, credits_charged, operation_id, webhook_received_at, created_at',
> 55	  },
> 56	};
> 57

> 59	  'eq',

> 69	];
> 70

> 73	export const FORBIDDEN_COLUMN_PATTERNS: readonly RegExp[] = [
> 74	  /access_token/i,

> 80	  /^phone$/i,

> 82	];

> 1	-- Revenue Snippet Refactor — Option 3 of .claude/proposals/pipeline-runs-revenue-fix-v1.md
> 2	-- Make mcoin_transactions the canonical ledger by having deduct_mco_coins log negative-amount rows.
> 3	-- Backwards-compatible: existing callers pass only (p_user_id, p_amount); new params default.
> 4	
> 5	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.deduct_mco_coins(
> 6	  p_user_id uuid,
> 7	  p_amount  decimal,
> 8	  p_action  text  DEFAULT 'spend',
> 9	  p_context jsonb DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb
> 10	)
> 11	RETURNS void
> 12	LANGUAGE plpgsql
> 13	SECURITY DEFINER
> 14	SET search_path = public
> 15	AS $$
> 16	BEGIN
> 17	  UPDATE profiles
> 18	     SET mco_balance = GREATEST(0, mco_balance - p_amount)
> 19	   WHERE id = p_user_id
> 20	     AND mco_balance >= p_amount;
> 21	
> 22	  IF NOT FOUND THEN
> 23	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Saldo mcoCoins insuficiente para completar a operação';
> 24	  END IF;
> 25	
> 26	  -- Ledger entry. amount stored as NEGATIVE for spends (mirrors award_mco_coins positive).
> 27	  -- mcoin_transactions.amount is INTEGER; cast required since p_amount is decimal.
> 28	  INSERT INTO mcoin_transactions (user_id, action, amount, context)
> 29	  VALUES (p_user_id, p_action, -p_amount::integer, p_context);
> 30	END;
> 31	$$;
> 32	
> 33	GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.deduct_mco_coins(uuid, decimal) TO authenticated;
> 34	GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.deduct_mco_coins(uuid, decimal, text, jsonb) TO authenticated;
> 35

> **Tool Result (Read)** - ✅ Success
>
> ```
> 1	# SOP — mcoCoins Ledger Reconciliation
> 2	
> 3	**Versão:** v1 · **Selada:** 2026-05-16 · **Lei 2 (Processo Antecipado)**
> 4	
> 5	## ORO triplet
> 6	
> 7	- **Operator:** scientist agent ou Sovereign
> 8	- **Reviewer:** Sovereign
> 9	- **Owner:** Sovereign até v6.4.x; depois CFO/Finance role quando existir
> 10	
> 11	## Contexto
> 12	
> 13	A partir de `20260516224541_deduct_mco_coins_ledger.sql` (deploy 2026-05-16):
> 14	- `award_mco_coins` → INSERT linha positiva em `mcoin_transactions`
> 15	- `deduct_mco_coins` → INSERT linha negativa em `mcoin_transactions` + UPDATE `profiles.mco_balance`
> 16	
> 17	Antes desta migration, `deduct` só atualizava balance sem logar. Resultado: histórico pré-2026-05-16 é caixa-preta contábil — balance ≠ Σ ledger.
> 18	
> 19	Audit v1 confirmou: Sovereign user `ada39fae...` tem `mco_balance=5533` e zero linhas em `mcoin_transactions`. Reconciliação retroativa requer decisão Sovereign (backfill sintético vs aceitar opening balance).
> 20	
> 21	## Sequence — execução manual humana
> 22	
> 23	| # | Action | Output esperado | Verification gate |
> 24	|---|--------|-----------------|-------------------|
> 25	| 1 | Query opening balance por user: `SELECT id, mco_balance FROM profiles` | Lista de UUIDs + balances atuais | Count > 0 |
> 26	| 2 | Query ledger sum por user: `SELECT user_id, SUM(amount) FROM mcoin_transactions GROUP BY user_id` | Lista de UUIDs + sum | Pode ser vazio em deploy novo |
> 27	| 3 | Computar drift = `mco_balance - SUM(amount)` por user | Tabela `user_id, drift` | Drift = 0 → reconciled; drift ≠ 0 → backfill needed |
> 28	| 4 | (Se drift ≠ 0) Decidir: opção A = INSERT linha sintética `action='opening_balance_2026_05_16'` com amount=drift; opção B = aceitar drift como pre-ledger black-box | Decisão registrada em ADR | ADR mergeado em `docs/bok/<slug>/05-sdd.md` |
> 29	| 5 | (Se opção A) Executar inserts sintéticos via SQL com prefix `opening_balance_` em action | Linhas inseridas com timestamp '2026-05-16T00:00:00Z' | `SUM(amount) = mco_balance` per user |
> 30	| 6 | Re-rodar drift check | Drift = 0 ∀ user | Reconciled |
> 31	| 7 | Agendar reconciliação semanal: `scripts/mcoins-reconcile.ts` cron `0 4 * * 0` (domingo 04:00) | Cron line installed | `crontab -l` mostra a linha |
> 32	
> 33	## Verification gates
> 34	
> 35	- Step 3: drift ZERO → reconciled. Drift ≠ 0 sem decisão registrada → STOP.
> 36	- Step 5: SUM must equal balance after backfill. Se diferir, rollback (DELETE das linhas sintéticas inseridas).
> 37	
> 38	## Recovery path
> 39	
> 40	- **Backfill quebrou balance**: ROLLBACK transactional não disponível em INSERTs separados. Fix: `DELETE FROM mcoin_transactions WHERE action LIKE 'opening_balance_%' AND created_at = '2026-05-16T00:00:00Z'` + re-rodar do Step 4.
> 41	- **Drift descoberto pós-Phase-Commercial**: bloquear novos creditos do user até reconciliar. Notificar Sovereign + Reviewer.
> 42	
> 43	## Success signal
> 44	
> 45	- `SELECT (mco_balance - COALESCE(SUM(amount),0)) AS drift FROM profiles p LEFT JOIN mcoin_transactions t ON t.user_id=p.id GROUP BY p.id, p.mco_balance` retorna `drift=0` ∀ row.
> 46	- `infra_health_logs` mostra entry `service='mcoins-reconcile'`, status='healthy' recente.
> 47	
> 48	## Anti-patterns
> 49	
> 50	- ❌ "Vou só ajustar o balance manualmente" — ledger fica de fora, drift volta na próxima query.
> 51	- ❌ INSERT sem `action` prefix `opening_balance_*` — vira ruído indistinguível de spend real.
> 52	- ❌ Aceitar drift "porque é pouco" — bom é zero ou registrado explicitamente.
> 53	
> 54	## Referências
> 55	
> 56	- `supabase/migrations/20260508100000_mcoin_transactions.sql`
> 57	- `supabase/migrations/20260516224541_deduct_mco_coins_ledger.sql`
> 58	- `.claude/context/survival-audit-v1.md` §3.B (Discrepância documentada)
> 59	
> ```

>
> ```
> 1	# SOP — SQL Bridge Admin Bootstrap
> 2	
> 3	**Versão:** v1 · **Selada:** 2026-05-16 · **Lei 2 (Processo Antecipado)**
> 4	
> 5	## ORO triplet
> 6	
> 7	- **Operator:** Sovereign (Gabriel) executando direto via Supabase Studio SQL Editor
> 8	- **Reviewer:** Sovereign (self-review — sensível, sem delegação)
> 9	- **Owner:** Sovereign (security boundary change)
> 10	
> 11	## Contexto
> 12	
> 13	`aios-sql-bridge` v1 requer admin role (`has_role(auth.uid(),'admin')`). Audit v1 mostrou que `user_roles` só contém roles `viewer` — nenhum admin existe. Sem admin, end-to-end do bridge é inacessível. Esta SOP é o **bootstrap manual seguro** para promover o Sovereign user.
> 14	
> 15	## Pre-conditions
> 16	
> 17	- Sovereign user_id: `ada39fae-67e1-4e53-af1c-5a18e1c108e8` (confirmar em `profiles`)
> 18	- Acesso ao Supabase Studio (https://supabase.com/dashboard/project/bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa)
> 19	- `user_roles` tem RLS — service-role bypass apenas via Studio SQL Editor ou via psql com service-role key
> 20	
> 21	## Sequence — execução manual humana
> 22	
> 23	| # | Action | Output esperado | Verification gate |
> 24	|---|--------|-----------------|-------------------|
> 25	| 1 | Confirmar Sovereign user_id via `SELECT id, mco_balance FROM profiles ORDER BY mco_balance DESC LIMIT 5` | UUID `ada39fae...` no topo (highest balance) | Match string-by-string |
> 26	| 2 | Verificar app_role enum: `SELECT unnest(enum_range(NULL::app_role))` | Lista contém `'admin'` | `'admin'` presente |
> 27	| 3 | Confirmar que `ada39fae...` ainda não é admin: `SELECT * FROM user_roles WHERE user_id='ada39fae-67e1-4e53-af1c-5a18e1c108e8' AND role='admin'` | Zero rows | Empty result |
> 28	| 4 | INSERT do role admin: `INSERT INTO user_roles (user_id, role) VALUES ('ada39fae-67e1-4e53-af1c-5a18e1c108e8','admin') RETURNING id, user_id, role, created_at` | UUID novo, role='admin', timestamp atual | Material proof: UUID returned |
> 29	| 5 | Verificar via has_role: `SELECT public.has_role('ada39fae-67e1-4e53-af1c-5a18e1c108e8','admin')` | `true` | Boolean true |
> 30	| 6 | Sovereign obtém JWT fresh (login no frontend ou `supabase.auth.signInWithPassword` no Studio) | JWT string ~800 chars | jwt.io decode mostra `sub: ada39fae...` |
> 31	| 7 | Smoke test: `curl POST aios-sql-bridge` com `Authorization: Bearer <jwt>` body `{"table":"profiles","limit":1}` | HTTP 200 + 1 row | `rows.length === 1` |
> 32	
> 33	## Verification gates
> 34	
> 35	- Step 4: INSERT RETURNING DEVE retornar o UUID. Se erro de duplicata, rollback (step 3 falhou) — investigar.
> 36	- Step 5: `has_role` retorna `true`. Se `false`, RLS pode estar bloqueando OR role não foi inserido. Verificar com `SELECT * FROM user_roles WHERE user_id=...`.
> 37	- Step 7: HTTP 200 com row real. Se 403 "Admin role required", role não foi propagado (cache JWT?). Sovereign refresh do JWT (logout/login).
> 38	
> 39	## Recovery path
> 40	
> 41	- **Step 4 falhou (RLS bloqueia INSERT)**: rodar via service-role no SQL Editor (Studio bypasses RLS). Se ainda falhar, ALTER TABLE user_roles DISABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY temporário (super-risky, reverter imediatamente após insert).
> 42	- **Step 7 retorna 401 "Invalid JWT"**: JWT expirou. Re-logar e tentar com token novo. JWT do Supabase tem ~1h de validade.
> 43	- **Step 7 retorna 429 "Daily quota exceeded"**: já consumiu 100 queries hoje. Esperar 24h OU `DELETE FROM usage_tracking WHERE user_id=... AND resource_type='sql_bridge_query' AND created_at > NOW() - INTERVAL '24h'` para reset.
> 44	
> 45	## Success signal
> 46	
> 47	- `SELECT * FROM user_roles WHERE user_id='ada39fae...' AND role='admin'` retorna 1 row
> 48	- Curl POST com JWT retorna `{"rows":[{...}], "rowCount":1, "queryHash":"...", "executedAt":"..."}`
> 49	- `infra_health_logs` mostra entry `service='aios-sql-bridge'`, status='healthy', timestamp ≤ 5s atrás
> 50	
> 51	## Anti-patterns
> 52	
> 53	- ❌ Promover múltiplos users a admin "preventivamente" — apenas Sovereign até Phase Commercial
> 54	- ❌ Deixar SQL Editor aberto com admin role após o smoke — fechar imediatamente
> 55	- ❌ Compartilhar JWT em chat — JWT é credencial; pode ser revogado mas custa fricção
> 56	
> 57	## Referências
> 58	
> 59	- `supabase/functions/aios-sql-bridge/index.ts:73-89` (admin gate logic)
> 60	- `supabase/migrations/20260402014040_b141fb0f-...sql:237-261` (has_role function definition)
> 61	- `.claude/context/survival-audit-v1.md` §3.A (No admin findings)
> 62	
> ```

> 1	# SOP — SQL Bridge Emergency Read-Only Fallback
> 2	
> 3	**Versão:** v1 · **Selada:** 2026-05-16 · **Lei 2 (Processo Antecipado)**
> 4	
> 5	## ORO triplet
> 6	
> 7	- **Operator:** Sovereign (Gabriel) — caminho que NÃO depende da AI nem do bridge funcionando
> 8	- **Reviewer:** Sovereign (self)
> 9	- **Owner:** Sovereign
> 10	
> 11	## Contexto
> 12	
> 13	Se `aios-sql-bridge` cair (deploy ruim, quota exhausted, admin role revogado, JWT expirado, gateway 5xx), a AI vira "Cérebro sem mãos" para Materiality proofs. Esta SOP é o **caminho manual de emergência** para o Sovereign obter UUIDs reais sem o bridge.
> 14	
> 15	## Sequence — execução manual humana
> 16	
> 17	| # | Action | Como executar | Verification gate |
> 18	|---|--------|---------------|-------------------|
> 19	| 1 | Abrir Supabase Studio: https://supabase.com/dashboard/project/bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa/sql/new | Browser | Login válido |
> 20	| 2 | Escolher query a partir do menu abaixo (canned queries) | Copy/paste | Query string completa |
> 21	| 3 | Executar (Run ou Ctrl+Enter) | Studio | Result panel mostra rows |
> 22	| 4 | Copiar UUIDs do result panel | Manual highlight + Ctrl+C | Lista de UUIDs em formato 8-4-4-4-12 |
> 23	| 5 | Colar no chat com AI (ou em log file) | Manual | AI usa como Material proof |
> 24	
> 25	## Canned queries (read-only, paste-and-run)
> 26	
> 27	### Q1 — Latest mcoin_transactions (10 UUIDs)
> 28	```sql
> 29	SELECT id, user_id, action, amount, created_at, context
> 30	FROM mcoin_transactions
> 31	ORDER BY created_at DESC
> 32	LIMIT 10;
> 33	```
> 34	
> 35	### Q2 — Sovereign balance + recent activity
> 36	```sql
> 37	SELECT p.id, p.mco_balance, p.score, p.updated_at,
> 38	       (SELECT count(*) FROM mcoin_transactions t WHERE t.user_id=p.id) AS ledger_rows
> 39	FROM profiles p
> 40	WHERE p.id = 'ada39fae-67e1-4e53-af1c-5a18e1c108e8';
> 41	```
> 42	
> 43	### Q3 — Canvas executions com status='failed' (últimas 20)
> 44	```sql
> 45	SELECT id, user_id, provider, model, status, credits_charged, error_message, created_at
> 46	FROM vm_canvas_executions
> 47	WHERE status = 'failed'
> 48	ORDER BY created_at DESC
> 49	LIMIT 20;
> 50	```
> 51	
> 52	### Q4 — Infra health timeseries (últimas 50)
> 53	```sql
> 54	SELECT service, status, last_seen_at
> 55	FROM infra_health_logs
> 56	ORDER BY last_seen_at DESC
> 57	LIMIT 50;
> 58	```
> 59	
> 60	### Q5 — Pipeline runs com revenue
> 61	```sql
> 62	SELECT id, user_id, topic, status, mco_cost, started_at, completed_at
> 63	FROM pipeline_runs
> 64	ORDER BY started_at DESC
> 65	LIMIT 20;
> 66	```
> 67	
> 68	### Q6 — Reconciliation drift por user
> 69	```sql
> 70	SELECT p.id,
> 71	       p.mco_balance,
> 72	       COALESCE(SUM(t.amount), 0) AS ledger_sum,
> 73	       p.mco_balance - COALESCE(SUM(t.amount), 0) AS drift
> 74	FROM profiles p
> 75	LEFT JOIN mcoin_transactions t ON t.user_id = p.id
> 76	GROUP BY p.id, p.mco_balance
> 77	ORDER BY ABS(p.mco_balance - COALESCE(SUM(t.amount), 0)) DESC;
> 78	```
> 79	
> 80	## Verification gates
> 81	
> 82	- Cada query deve retornar antes de 5s (Studio default timeout).
> 83	- Result panel mostra row count exato.
> 84	- UUIDs sempre em formato `xxxxxxxx-xxxx-xxxx-xxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx`.
> 85	
> 86	## Recovery path
> 87	
> 88	- **Studio retorna timeout:** simplificar query (LIMIT menor, remover JOIN). Se persistir, investigar via psql direto com pooler URL: `psql "$(cat supabase/.temp/pooler-url)"`.
> 89	- **Result vazio mas Sovereign esperava dados:** verificar filtros + RLS via `SET ROLE postgres;` no Studio (cuidado, role escalation).
> 90	- **Studio inacessível:** fallback ao psql via service-role key. Manual: `psql "postgresql://postgres.bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa:<password>@aws-0-...supabase.com:5432/postgres"` (password em `.env` ou vault).
> 91	
> 92	## Success signal
> 93	
> 94	- Result panel materializa UUIDs reais
> 95	- Sovereign pode copiá-los para validar claims da AI
> 96	- AI continua sem precisar do bridge (Lei 1 satisfeita via mão humana)
> 97	
> 98	## Anti-patterns
> 99	
> 100	- ❌ INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE direto no Studio sem migration — quebra a single source of truth do schema
> 101	- ❌ Compartilhar queries com PII em chat público
> 102	- ❌ Esquecer de fechar a aba Studio com role escalado
> 103	
> 104	## Referências
> 105	
> 106	- `supabase/.temp/pooler-url` (connection string fallback)
> 107	- `.env` (SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY)
> 108	- `supabase/functions/aios-sql-bridge/whitelist.ts` (tabelas suportadas pelo bridge equivalente)
> 109	
> ```

Logo, prova end-to-end direta de `user_roles` via bridge n\u00e3o \u00e9 poss\u00edvel sem extender whitelist.\n\n**Estrat\u00e9gia de duas pernas:**\n\n### 1.1 \u2014 Prova direta de `user_roles` via service-role REST (n\u00e3o usa bridge)\n```bash\nsource .env\ncurl -s \"https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/rest/v1/user_roles?user_id=eq.ada39fae-67e1-4e53-af1c-5a18e1c108e8&select=user_id,role,created_at\" \\\n  -H \"apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\" \\\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\"\n```\n**Expected material proof:** JSON array contendo `{user_id: \"ada39fae...\", role: \"admin\", created_at: \"<iso>\"}`. Se vazio \u2192 bootstrap n\u00e3o completou; abortar tudo e voltar para SOP `sql-bridge-admin-bootstrap.md`.\n\n### 1.2 \u2014 Prova indireta do bridge via `profiles` (gate admin)\nO simples fato de o bridge retornar 200 (vs 403 \"Admin role required for aios-sql-bridge\") comprova o admin gate. N\u00e3o preciso ler `user_roles` pelo bridge.\n\n```bash\ncurl -X POST https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/functions/v1/aios-sql-bridge \\\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer <ADMIN_JWT_DO_SOVEREIGN>\" \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"table\":\"profiles\",\"filters\":[{\"column\":\"id\",\"op\":\"eq\",\"value\":\"ada39fae-67e1-4e53-af1c-5a18e1c108e8\"}],\"limit\":1}'\n```\n**Expected:** HTTP 200 + `{rows:[{id,mco_balance,...}], rowCount:1, queryHash:\"<sha256>\", durationMs:<int>}`.\n\n**C\u00e9rebro sem m\u00e3os:** n\u00e3o tenho o JWT do Sovereign nesta sess\u00e3o. Para essa etapa preciso de UMA das a\u00e7\u00f5es abaixo:\n- (i) Sovereign cola o JWT fresh (Network tab no login.mcorch.com, header `Authorization`), OU\n- (ii) Sovereign roda o curl e cola o output JSON, OU\n- (iii) Sovereign autoriza extender o whitelist do bridge para `user_roles` (precisa editar `whitelist.ts`, redeploy, ent\u00e3o o bridge mostra a row admin diretamente \u2014 mas envolve code change).\n\n**Recomenda\u00e7\u00e3o:** (ii) \u2014 Sovereign roda o curl com seu JWT, cola response. Zero code change, zero risco de vazamento de JWT pela conversa, prova end-to-end v\u00e1lida. Vou pedir isso explicitamente.\n\n### Material artifacts a coletar\n- HTTP 200 do bridge + `queryHash` + `rowCount=1` + `durationMs<5000`\n- JSON com role='admin' para o user_id Sovereign\n- Confirma\u00e7\u00e3o ABERTA no chat: \"AIOS_SQL_BRIDGE OPERACIONAL EM `2026-05-17T<UTC>`\"\n\n---\n\n## Phase 2 \u2014 OE02: Ledger Reconciliation Insert (Lei 1 + Pillar 3)\n\n**Goal:** inserir 1 linha em `mcoin_transactions` levando o drift do Sovereign de `+5533` para `0`.\n\n**SOP de refer\u00eancia (j\u00e1 existe):** `docs/processes/mcoins-ledger-reconciliation.md` Step 4 Op\u00e7\u00e3o A. Sovereign j\u00e1 escolheu Op\u00e7\u00e3o A nesta diretiva (\"n\u00e3o aceitaremos o drift\").\n\n**Schema target (`20260508100000_mcoin_transactions.sql`):**\n- `id uuid PK default gen_random_uuid()`\n- `user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id)`\n- `action text NOT NULL` \u2190 `opening_balance_reconciliation`\n- `amount integer NOT NULL` \u2190 `5533`\n- `created_at timestamptz DEFAULT NOW()`\n- `context jsonb DEFAULT '{}'`\n- `project_id uuid` \u2190 null\n\n**Action:**\n```bash\nsource .env\ncurl -X POST \"https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/rest/v1/mcoin_transactions\" \\\n  -H \"apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\" \\\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\" \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -H \"Prefer: return=representation\" \\\n  -d '{\n    \"user_id\": \"ada39fae-67e1-4e53-af1c-5a18e1c108e8\",\n    \"action\": \"opening_balance_reconciliation\",\n    \"amount\": 5533,\n    \"context\": {\n      \"source\": \"SSP-01 OE02\",\n      \"directive\": \"Reconcilia\u00e7\u00e3o do Ledger \u2014 eliminar estado de fraude\",\n      \"reconciled_at\": \"2026-05-17\",\n      \"drift_before\": 5533,\n      \"drift_after\": 0,\n      \"operator\": \"MCORCH Master Execution Agent\",\n      \"reviewer\": \"Sovereign\",\n      \"owner\": \"Sovereign\",\n      \"audit_ref\": \".claude/context/survival-audit-v1.md \u00a73.B\",\n      \"sop_ref\": \"docs/processes/mcoins-ledger-reconciliation.md Step 5\"\n    }\n  }'\n```\n**Materiality proof:** RETURNING JSON com `id: \"<uuid>\"`, `amount: 5533`, `action: \"opening_balance_reconciliation\"`, `created_at: \"<iso>\"`.\n\n**Drift verification (Step 6 da SOP):**\n```bash\ncurl -s \"https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/rest/v1/mcoin_transactions?user_id=eq.ada39fae-67e1-4e53-af1c-5a18e1c108e8&select=action,amount,created_at&order=created_at.desc\" \\\n  -H \"apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\"\n```\nExpected: array com a linha rec\u00e9m-inserida + soma dos amounts = 5533 = mco_balance do Sovereign \u2192 **drift = 0**.\n\n**N\u00e3o usar RPC `award_mco_coins`** \u2014 ela adiciona ao balance (errado aqui, balance j\u00e1 \u00e9 5533) e tem cap de 1000/award. Direct INSERT via service-role \u00e9 o caminho.\n\n**Por que isto sobe Pillar 3:** materializa o ledger hist\u00f3rico em vez de aceitar caixa-preta. Vira fundamento para o daily cap (Phase 4) que ser\u00e1 query-able sobre o ledger.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 3 \u2014 OE03: Refator Circadiano (Lei 2 \u2014 SOP antes do code)\n\n**Goal:** trocar 2 linhas de cron (02:30 + 03:30) por 3 linhas (02:00 + 03:00 + 04:00) representando arquitetura de sono. Manter cron nativo OpenClaw \u00e0s 03:00 (paralelo a Stage 2, sem conflito \u2014 sistemas independentes).\n\n### 3.1 SOP first: `docs/processes/nightly-circadian-cycle.md`\n\nCont\u00e9m ORO triplet + Operator/Sequence/Verification/Recovery/Success matching template das 4 SOPs j\u00e1 existentes. Cobre as 3 stages, depend\u00eancias entre elas, fallback se uma stage falhar (pr\u00f3xima ainda roda \u2014 desacopladas por hora).\n\n### 3.2 Stage 1 (02:00 \u2014 Light Sleep) \u2014 `scripts/nightly-stage1-light-sleep.sh` (novo, ~60 LOC)\n\nMirror do padr\u00e3o de `nightly-bridge-refresh.sh` (escalate_failure + trap EXIT + log apenso):\n- **Log rotation:** rotacionar `/var/log/mcorch-*.log` (manter \u00faltimos 7 dias, gzip resto). Usar `logrotate -f` configurando se necess\u00e1rio, ou `find ... -mtime +7 -exec gzip {} \\;`.\n- **Temp cleanup:** `find /tmp/openclaw-incidents -mtime +7 -delete` + `find /tmp/mcorch-*.log -mtime +14 -delete` + `find /tmp/morning-briefing-tg-response*.json -mtime +3 -delete`.\n- **Infra spot check:** `docker ps --filter \"name=mcorch\"` + `curl -s http://localhost:8001/api/v2/heartbeat` + insert linha em `infra_health_logs` com `service='nightly-stage1'`, `status='healthy'|'unhealthy'`.\n\n### 3.3 Stage 2 (03:00 \u2014 Deep Sleep) \u2014 `scripts/nightly-stage2-deep-sleep.sh` (novo, ~40 LOC wrapper)\n\n- Chama `scripts/nightly-bridge-refresh.sh` (mantido como-est\u00e1 \u2014 bridge + distribute + reindex).\n- **Ledger drift check** (read-only, alert-only): query `SELECT p.id, p.mco_balance - COALESCE(SUM(t.amount),0) AS drift FROM profiles p LEFT JOIN mcoin_transactions t ON t.user_id=p.id GROUP BY p.id, p.mco_balance HAVING drift != 0` via psql. Se houver rows \u2192 log WARN + insert `infra_health_logs` `service='ledger-drift-watch'` `status='unhealthy'` com lista de user_ids drift. **N\u00e3o auto-backfill** \u2014 reconcilia\u00e7\u00e3o requer decis\u00e3o Sovereign por SOP.\n- **DB compact** (opcional, baixa frequ\u00eancia): `VACUUM ANALYZE mcorch_nodes; VACUUM ANALYZE mcoin_transactions; VACUUM ANALYZE vm_canvas_executions;` via psql.\n- **OpenClaw native cron** (`Memory Dreaming Promotion` em `~/.openclaw/cron/jobs.json`) continua \u00e0s 03:00 \u2014 roda em paralelo, no daemon pr\u00f3prio. Sem mudan\u00e7a.\n\n### 3.4 Stage 3 (04:00 \u2014 REM Cycle) \u2014 `scripts/nightly-stage3-rem.sh` (novo, ~30 LOC wrapper)\n\n- Chama `scripts/news-impact-analyzer.ts` (NOVO \u2014 ver 3.5).\n- Chama `scripts/morning-briefing.sh` (mantido como-est\u00e1 mas o compose agora l\u00ea o latest `news_pulse` node \u2014 ver 3.6).\n\n### 3.5 News Impact Analyzer \u2014 `scripts/news-impact-analyzer.ts` (novo, ~150 LOC)\n\nBun script. Pipeline:\n1. Firecrawl `search` em 2-3 queries fixas: `[\"AI agents news\", \"Anthropic Claude Brasil\", \"ag\u00eancia IA marketing crescimento\"]`. Cada query \u2192 top 3 resultados.\n2. Para cada result, extrair `title + snippet` (sem scrape de p\u00e1gina inteira \u2014 economiza cr\u00e9ditos).\n3. Compor prompt para OpenRouter (modelo `anthropic/claude-haiku-4-5` para custo) com instru\u00e7\u00e3o: \"destile 3-5 bullets em PT-BR relevantes para MCORCH (ag\u00eancia IA, mcoCoins, Canvas Studio, automa\u00e7\u00e3o de conte\u00fado). Cada bullet \u2264120 chars. Nada de hype.\"\n4. Insert node em `mcorch_nodes` com `node_type='news_pulse'`, `content=<bullets-markdown>`, `metadata={ headlines: [{title, url, source}, ...], generated_at, model }`.\n5. Trigger `embed-mcorch-node` aut\u00f4nomo (j\u00e1 existe trigger INSERT).\n6. Output stdout: o markdown dos bullets (consumido por compose-morning-briefing via stdin ou lendo o n\u00f3).\n\n**Failure path:** Firecrawl/OpenRouter falha \u2192 log WARN + skip inser\u00e7\u00e3o + Stage 3 continua para morning-briefing (briefing sem se\u00e7\u00e3o News Pulse). N\u00c3O escalate failure (Stage 3 \u00e9 best-effort).\n\n**Estimativa de custo:** 3 Firecrawl searches \u00d7 ~$0.01 + 1 LLM call ~500 tokens = ~$0.05/dia. Sustent\u00e1vel.\n\n### 3.6 Update `scripts/compose-morning-briefing.ts`\n\nAdicionar se\u00e7\u00e3o \"\ud83d\udcf0 News Pulse\" no markdown composto. L\u00ea o latest node `node_type='news_pulse'` via Supabase REST (j\u00e1 tem pattern em `revenueSnippet()`).\n\n### 3.7 Crontab swap\n\n**Remover** (ap\u00f3s dry-run OK):\n```\n30 2 * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/nightly-bridge-refresh.sh\n30 3 * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/morning-briefing.sh\n```\n\n**Adicionar:**\n```\n0 2 * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/nightly-stage1-light-sleep.sh >> /var/log/mcorch-stage1.log 2>&1\n0 3 * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/nightly-stage2-deep-sleep.sh >> /var/log/mcorch-stage2.log 2>&1\n0 4 * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/nightly-stage3-rem.sh >> /var/log/mcorch-stage3.log 2>&1\n```\n\n**Watchdog/auto-publish/audit-4cs/ingest-codebase crons:** preservados sem altera\u00e7\u00e3o.\n\n**Pre-deploy gate:** `bash -n` em cada novo script + smoke-run manual de Stage 1 (mais isolado, sem deps externos) antes de editar crontab.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 4 \u2014 OE04: Daily Cap + Balance Toast + Re-audit\n\n### 4.1 SOP first: `docs/processes/canvas-daily-cap-handling.md`\n\nCobre: o que acontece quando cap \u00e9 atingido (HTTP 402 + JSON estruturado), como o user recupera (esperar janela 24h sliding), como Sovereign override (futuro \u2014 Phase 5), rationale do threshold (100 mcoCoins/dia \u2248 \\$8 max Canvas spend ao markup 13\u00d7).\n\n### 4.2 Backend: Daily Cap em `canvas-execute`\n\n**File:** `supabase/functions/canvas-execute/index.ts`\n\n**Insert ANTES do block \"Check user credits\" (linha ~122):**\n```typescript\n// SSP-01 OE04 \u2014 Daily cap enforcement (100 mcoCoins/day Canvas spend per user)\n// Reads mcoin_transactions ledger (canonical post-v6.4.0); fail-open on query error\n// to preserve user experience if observability layer is degraded.\nconst DAILY_CAP_MCOCOINS = 100;\nconst dayAgo = new Date(Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();\nconst { data: spendRows, error: spendErr } = await admin\n  .from(\"mcoin_transactions\")\n  .select(\"amount\")\n  .eq(\"user_id\", user.id)\n  .like(\"action\", \"canvas_%\")\n  .gte(\"created_at\", dayAgo);\n\nif (spendErr) {\n  console.error(\"[canvas-execute] daily cap query error:\", spendErr);\n}\n\nconst spentToday = (spendRows ?? []).reduce(\n  (sum, r) => sum + Math.abs(Number(r.amount) || 0),\n  0,\n);\n\nif (spentToday + creditCost > DAILY_CAP_MCOCOINS) {\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify({\n    error: \"Daily Canvas cap reached\",\n    cap: DAILY_CAP_MCOCOINS,\n    spent_today: spentToday,\n    required: creditCost,\n    resets_at: new Date(Date.now() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),\n  }), { status: 402, headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" } });\n}\n```\n\n**Update do `deduct_mco_coins` call (linha ~253)** \u2014 usar action tag espec\u00edfico (essencial para o cap query funcionar):\n```typescript\nawait admin.rpc(\"deduct_mco_coins\", {\n  p_user_id: user.id,\n  p_amount: creditCost,\n  p_action: body.node_type === \"image_to_video\" ? \"canvas_video_spend\" : \"canvas_image_spend\",\n  p_context: {\n    execution_id: execution.id,\n    provider: body.provider,\n    model: body.model,\n    node_type: body.node_type,\n  },\n});\n```\n\n**Cleanup do fallback de update direto** (linhas 256-260) \u2014 agora redundante com RPC tendo ledger. Remover.\n\n**Deploy:** `npx supabase functions deploy canvas-execute`\n\n### 4.3 Frontend: Balance Toast em `DashboardLayout`\n\n**File:** `src/components/dashboard/DashboardLayout.tsx`\n\nAdd imports + constant:\n```typescript\nimport { toast } from 'sonner';\nconst LOW_BALANCE_THRESHOLD = 50;\n```\n\nUpdate initial load (linha ~52):\n```typescript\n.then(({ data }) => {\n  const balance = data?.mco_balance ?? 0;\n  setMcoBalance(balance);\n  if (balance < LOW_BALANCE_THRESHOLD) {\n    toast.warning(`Saldo baixo: ${balance} mcoCoins`, {\n      description: 'Recarregue para continuar gerando conte\u00fado no Canvas.',\n      duration: 6000,\n      id: 'low-balance', // dedupe\n    });\n  }\n});\n```\n\nUpdate Realtime callback (linha ~57):\n```typescript\n(payload) => {\n  const newBalance = (payload.new as any).mco_balance ?? 0;\n  setMcoBalance((prev) => {\n    if (prev !== null && prev >= LOW_BALANCE_THRESHOLD && newBalance < LOW_BALANCE_THRESHOLD) {\n      toast.warning(`Saldo baixo: ${newBalance} mcoCoins`, {\n        description: 'Recarregue para continuar gerando conte\u00fado no Canvas.',\n        duration: 8000,\n        id: 'low-balance',\n      });\n    }\n    return newBalance;\n  });\n}\n```\n\nVisual indicator (pill bg amber quando baixo):\n```typescript\n{mcoBalance !== null && (\n  <div className={`hidden sm:flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold ${\n    mcoBalance < LOW_BALANCE_THRESHOLD\n      ? 'bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 text-amber-600'\n      : 'bg-primary/10 border border-primary/20 text-primary'\n  }`}>\n    <Coins className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />\n    {mcoBalance} <span className=\"font-normal opacity-70\">mcoCoins</span>\n  </div>\n)}\n```\n\n**Verify:** `npx tsc --noEmit` (zero erros) + `bun run build` (chunk DashboardLayout n\u00e3o cresce >2kB).\n\n### 4.4 Re-audit Survival v2\n\nCriar `.claude/context/survival-audit-v2.md` espelhando estrutura do v1. > - Confirmação ABERTA no chat: "AIOS_SQL_BRIDGE OPERACIONAL EM `2026-05-17T<UTC>`"

> ## Phase 2 — OE02: Ledger Reconciliation Insert (Lei 1 + Pillar 3)
> 
> **Goal:** inserir 1 linha em `mcoin_transactions` levando o drift do Sovereign de `+5533` para `0`.
> 
> **SOP de referência (já existe):** `docs/processes/mcoins-ledger-reconciliation.md` Step 4 Opção A. Sovereign já escolheu Opção A nesta diretiva ("não aceitaremos o drift").
> 
> **Schema target (`20260508100000_mcoin_transactions.sql`):**
> - `id uuid PK default gen_random_uuid()`
> - `user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id)`
> - `action text NOT NULL` ← `opening_balance_reconciliation`
> - `amount integer NOT NULL` ← `5533`
> - `created_at timestamptz DEFAULT NOW()`
> - `context jsonb DEFAULT '{}'`
> - `project_id uuid` ← null
> 
> **Action:**
> ```bash
> source .env
> curl -X POST "https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/rest/v1/mcoin_transactions" \
>   -H "apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" \
>   -H "Authorization: Bearer $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" \
>   -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
>   -H "Prefer: return=representation" \
>   -d '{
>     "user_id": "ada39fae-67e1-4e53-af1c-5a18e1c108e8",
>     "action": "opening_balance_reconciliation",
>     "amount": 5533,
>     "context": {
>       "source": "SSP-01 OE02",
>       "directive": "Reconciliação do Ledger — eliminar estado de fraude",
>       "reconciled_at": "2026-05-17",
>       "drift_before": 5533,
>       "drift_after": 0,
>       "operator": "MCORCH Master Execution Agent",
>       "reviewer": "Sovereign",
>       "owner": "Sovereign",
>       "audit_ref": ".claude/context/survival-audit-v1.md §3.B",
>       "sop_ref": "docs/processes/mcoins-ledger-reconciliation.md Step 5"
>     }
>   }'
> ```
> **Materiality proof:** RETURNING JSON com `id: "<uuid>"`, `amount: 5533`, `action: "opening_balance_reconciliation"`, `created_at: "<iso>"`.
> 
> **Drift verification (Step 6 da SOP):**
> ```bash
> curl -s "https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/rest/v1/mcoin_transactions?user_id=eq.ada39fae-67e1-4e53-af1c-5a18e1c108e8&select=action,amount,created_at&order=created_at.desc" \
>   -H "apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"
> ```
> Expected: array com a linha recém-inserida + soma dos amounts = 5533 = mco_balance do Sovereign → **drift = 0**.
> 
> **Não usar RPC `award_mco_coins`** — ela adiciona ao balance (errado aqui, balance já é 5533) e tem cap de 1000/award. Direct INSERT via service-role é o caminho.
> 
> **Por que isto sobe Pillar 3:** materializa o ledger histórico em vez de aceitar caixa-preta. Vira fundamento para o daily cap (Phase 4) que será query-able sobre o ledger.
>

> ## Phase 3 — OE03: Refator Circadiano v1.0 (Lei 2 — SOP antes do code)
> 
> **Goal:** trocar 2 linhas de cron (02:30 + 03:30) por 3 linhas espaçadas 2h em **horário BRT** (03:00 + 05:00 + 07:00) representando arquitetura de sono com isolamento de CPU/IO. Manter cron nativo OpenClaw às 03:00 (sobrepõe Stage 1 — sistemas independentes, sem conflito; OpenClaw daemon próprio).
> 
> **Pré-condição (verificar antes de tocar crontab):** confirmar `timedatectl` mostra `America/Sao_Paulo` (= BRT) ou outro fuso. Se server estiver em UTC, converter:
> - 03:00 BRT = 06:00 UTC → `0 6 * * *`
> - 05:00 BRT = 08:00 UTC → `0 8 * * *`
> - 07:00 BRT = 10:00 UTC → `0 10 * * *`
> 
> Crons existentes (02:30 e 03:30) sugerem timezone local = BRT (caso contrário, 02:30 UTC = 23:30 BRT do dia anterior, o que não bate com o "nightly" semântico). Comando de verificação obrigatório antes do swap: `timedatectl | grep -E "Time zone|Local time"`.
> 
> ### 3.1 SOP first: `docs/processes/nightly-circadian-cycle.md`
> 
> Contém ORO triplet + Operator/Sequence/Verification/Recovery/Success matching template das 4 SOPs já existentes. Cobre as 3 stages, dependências entre elas, fallback se uma stage falhar (próxima ainda roda — desacopladas por hora).
> 
> ### 3.2 Stage 1 (03:00 BRT — Light Sleep) — `scripts/nightly-stage1-light-sleep.sh` (novo, ~60 LOC)
> 
> **Foco:** IO (cleanup + rotação) — operação não-CPU-intensiva. Roda em paralelo do OpenClaw `Memory Dreaming Promotion` (mesmo 03:00 BRT, daemon próprio). Sem conflito porque cada um toca arquivos distintos.
> 
> Mirror do padrão de `nightly-bridge-refresh.sh` (escalate_failure + trap EXIT + log apenso):
> - **Log rotation:** rotacionar `/var/log/mcorch-*.log` (manter últimos 7 dias, gzip resto). Usar `logrotate -f` configurando se necessário, ou `find ... -mtime +7 -exec gzip {} \;`.
> - **Temp cleanup:** `find /tmp/openclaw-incidents -mtime +7 -delete` + `find /tmp/mcorch-*.log -mtime +14 -delete` + `find /tmp/morning-briefing-tg-response*.json -mtime +3 -delete`.
> - **Infra spot check:** `docker ps --filter "name=mcorch"` + `curl -s http://localhost:8001/api/v2/heartbeat` + insert linha em `infra_health_logs` com `service='nightly-stage1'`, `status='healthy'|'unhealthy'`.
> 
> ### 3.3 Stage 2 (05:00 BRT — Deep Sleep) — `scripts/nightly-stage2-deep-sleep.sh` (novo, ~40 LOC wrapper)
> 
> **Foco:** Integridade — operações CPU/IO pesadas (reindex + VACUUM). Espaçamento de 2h do Stage 1 garante que cleanup terminou + thermal recovery do servidor + buffers liberados.
> 
> - Chama `scripts/nightly-bridge-refresh.sh` (mantido como-está — bridge + distribute + reindex). Era 02:30 antes; agora consolidado no Stage 2.
> - **Ledger drift check** (read-only, alert-only): query `SELECT p.id, p.mco_balance - COALESCE(SUM(t.amount),0) AS drift FROM profiles p LEFT JOIN mcoin_transactions t ON t.user_id=p.id GROUP BY p.id, p.mco_balance HAVING drift != 0` via psql. Se houver rows → log WARN + insert `infra_health_logs` `service='ledger-drift-watch'` `status='unhealthy'` com lista de user_ids drift. **Não auto-backfill** — reconciliação requer decisão Sovereign por SOP.
> - **DB compact** (opcional, baixa frequência): `VACUUM ANALYZE mcorch_nodes; VACUUM ANALYZE mcoin_transactions; VACUUM ANALYZE vm_canvas_executions;` via psql. Smoke-run primeiro: se VACUUM em `mcorch_nodes` (8307 rows) demorar >5min, defer para weekly cron (`0 5 * * 0` domingo).
> 
> ### 3.4 Stage 3 (07:00 BRT — REM Cycle) — `scripts/nightly-stage3-rem.sh` (novo, ~30 LOC wrapper)
> 
> **Foco:** Inteligência — síntese e entrega. 07:00 BRT alinha com despertar do Sovereign (briefing já no Telegram quando ele abrir o app).
> 
> - Chama `scripts/news-impact-analyzer.ts` (NOVO — ver 3.5).
> - Chama `scripts/morning-briefing.sh` (mantido como-está mas o compose agora lê o latest `news_pulse` node — ver 3.6). Era 03:30 antes; agora 07:00 BRT (briefing chega bem no horário de leitura humano).
> 
> ### 3.5 News Impact Analyzer — `scripts/news-impact-analyzer.ts` (novo, ~150 LOC)
> 
> Bun script. Pipeline:
> 1. Firecrawl `search` em 2-3 queries fixas: `["AI agents news", "Anthropic Claude Brasil", "agência IA marketing crescimento"]`. Cada query → top 3 resultados.
> 2. Para cada result, extrair `title + snippet` (sem scrape de página inteira — economiza créditos).
> 3. Compor prompt para OpenRouter (modelo `anthropic/claude-haiku-4-5` para custo) com instrução: "destile 3-5 bullets em PT-BR relevantes para MCORCH (agência IA, mcoCoins, Canvas Studio, automação de conteúdo). Cada bullet ≤120 chars. Nada de hype."
> 4. Insert node em `mcorch_nodes` com `node_type='news_pulse'`, `content=<bullets-markdown>`, `metadata={ headlines: [{title, url, source}, ...], generated_at, model }`.
> 5. Trigger `embed-mcorch-node` autônomo (já existe trigger INSERT).
> 6. Output stdout: o markdown dos bullets (consumido por compose-morning-briefing via stdin ou lendo o nó).
> 
> **Failure path:** Firecrawl/OpenRouter falha → log WARN + skip inserção + Stage 3 continua para morning-briefing (briefing sem seção News Pulse). NÃO escalate failure (Stage 3 é best-effort).
> 
> **Estimativa de custo:** 3 Firecrawl searches × ~$0.01 + 1 LLM call ~500 tokens = ~$0.05/dia. Sustentável.
> 
> ### 3.6 Update `scripts/compose-morning-briefing.ts`
> 
> Adicionar seção "📰 News Pulse" no markdown composto. Lê o latest node `node_type='news_pulse'` via Supabase REST (já tem pattern em `revenueSnippet()`).
> 
> ### 3.7 Crontab swap
> 
> **Step 0 (obrigatório):** verificar timezone do server.
> ```bash
> timedatectl | grep -E "Time zone|Local time"
> date  # cross-check
> ```
> - Se output mostra `Time zone: America/Sao_Paulo (-03)` → usar horários BRT diretos abaixo.
> - Se mostra `Time zone: Etc/UTC` → converter (+3h cada slot): `0 6` / `0 8` / `0 10`.
> 
> **Remover** (após dry-run OK):
> ```
> 30 2 * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/nightly-bridge-refresh.sh
> 30 3 * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/morning-briefing.sh
> ```
> 
> **Adicionar (assumindo timezone = BRT/America/Sao_Paulo):**
> ```
> 0 3 * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/nightly-stage1-light-sleep.sh >> /var/log/mcorch-stage1.log 2>&1
> 0 5 * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/nightly-stage2-deep-sleep.sh >> /var/log/mcorch-stage2.log 2>&1
> 0 7 * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/nightly-stage3-rem.sh >> /var/log/mcorch-stage3.log 2>&1
> ```
> 
> **Se server em UTC:**
> ```
> 0 6 * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/nightly-stage1-light-sleep.sh >> /var/log/mcorch-stage1.log 2>&1
> 0 8 * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/nightly-stage2-deep-sleep.sh >> /var/log/mcorch-stage2.log 2>&1
> 0 10 * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/nightly-stage3-rem.sh >> /var/log/mcorch-stage3.log 2>&1
> ```
> 
> **Watchdog/auto-publish/audit-4cs/ingest-codebase crons:** preservados sem alteração.
> 
> **OpenClaw native cron (`Memory Dreaming Promotion` em `~/.openclaw/cron/jobs.json` schedule `0 3 * * *`):** sem mudança. Sobrepõe Stage 1 — daemon próprio, IO próprio, sem conflito.
> 
> **Pre-deploy gate:** `bash -n` em cada novo script + smoke-run manual de Stage 1 (mais isolado, sem deps externos) antes de editar crontab + confirmação humana do `timedatectl` output.
>

> **Insert ANTES do block "Check user credits" (linha ~122):**
> ```typescript
> // SSP-01 OE04 — Daily cap enforcement (100 mcoCoins/day Canvas spend per user)
> // Reads mcoin_transactions ledger (canonical post-v6.4.0); fail-open on query error
> // to preserve user experience if observability layer is degraded.
> const DAILY_CAP_MCOCOINS = 100;
> const dayAgo = new Date(Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
> const { data: spendRows, error: spendErr } = await admin
>   .from("mcoin_transactions")
>   .select("amount")
>   .eq("user_id", user.id)
>   .like("action", "canvas_%")
>   .gte("created_at", dayAgo);
> 
> if (spendErr) {
>   console.error("[canvas-execute] daily cap query error:", spendErr);
> }
> 
> const spentToday = (spendRows ?? []).reduce(
>   (sum, r) => sum + Math.abs(Number(r.amount) || 0),
>   0,
> );
> 
> if (spentToday + creditCost > DAILY_CAP_MCOCOINS) {
>   return new Response(JSON.stringify({
>     error: "Daily Canvas cap reached",
>     cap: DAILY_CAP_MCOCOINS,
>     spent_today: spentToday,
>     required: creditCost,
>     resets_at: new Date(Date.now() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
>   }), { status: 402, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
> }
> ```

> });

> ## Verification end-to-end (Phase 5 — pré-seal)
> 
> ```bash
> # OE01 — admin proof material
> source .env
> curl -s "https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/rest/v1/user_roles?user_id=eq.ada39fae-67e1-4e53-af1c-5a18e1c108e8&select=*" \
>   -H "apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" \
>   -H "Authorization: Bearer $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"
> # Expected: array com role='admin'
> 
> # OE01 — bridge end-to-end (executado pelo Sovereign com seu JWT, output colado)
> # Expected: HTTP 200 + rows + queryHash
> 
> # OE02 — drift after reconcile
> curl -s "https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/rest/v1/mcoin_transactions?user_id=eq.ada39fae-67e1-4e53-af1c-5a18e1c108e8&select=action,amount&order=created_at.desc" \
>   -H "apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"
> # Expected: linha opening_balance_reconciliation amount=5533 + SUM=5533
> 
> # OE03 — script syntax + cron
> bash -n scripts/nightly-stage1-light-sleep.sh
> bash -n scripts/nightly-stage2-deep-sleep.sh
> bash -n scripts/nightly-stage3-rem.sh
> crontab -l | grep "nightly-stage"
> # Smoke run Stage 1 isolado
> bash scripts/nightly-stage1-light-sleep.sh && tail -20 /var/log/mcorch-stage1.log
> 
> # OE04 — typecheck + build + deploy
> npx tsc --noEmit
> bun run build
> npx supabase functions deploy canvas-execute
> # Material: deploy output mostra script size + URL
> 
> # Re-audit — manual scoring contra rubrica v1
> # Save: .claude/context/survival-audit-v2.md
> ```
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Out of scope (explícito — não fazer nesta sessão)
> 
> - **Canvas Phase 4.2c implementation em si:** apenas DESBLOQUEIA. Implementação (fork canvas-execute para video async + UI wiring + watchdog polling) é next-session.
> - **Backfill de ledger para outros users** (`924cb32e`, `3b88b71c`, `0b4d79d2`): OE02 fala apenas do Sovereign. Outros 2 têm balance≠0 com drift — out of scope, registrar como OTD-V660-001 (RPN ~120, owner scientist agent).
> - **News Impact Analyzer breadth:** começa com 3 Firecrawl queries + 3 bullets. Não é uma plataforma de news intelligence — é o MVP funcional. Expansão (mais queries, fontes específicas, classificação por relevância scoring) é Phase 7+.
> - **DB compact (VACUUM)** em Stage 2: pode ser custoso em prod. Se VACUUM ANALYZE em `mcorch_nodes` (10k+ linhas) demorar >5min, defer para weekly cron separado. Decisão data-driven no smoke-run.
> - **Extender whitelist do bridge** para `user_roles`: opcional. Só se Sovereign achar valor em poder consultar roles via bridge no futuro. Não bloqueia nada do plano.
> - **Admin cap-bypass** (override do daily cap para admins): Phase 5 / v6.7.0+.
> - **Reembolso automático em falhas de providers:** Phase 5 / v6.7.0+.
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Risk register
> 
> | Risk | Likelihood | Mitigation |
> |------|------------|------------|
> | Sovereign não consegue/quer colar JWT → OE01 fica parcial | Medium | Prova material via service-role direto cobre 80% (user_roles read). Bridge end-to-end fica como "verified by Sovereign, log only" no audit v2. |
> | `mcoin_transactions` INSERT trigger algum behavior inesperado | Low | RLS read-only ("mcoin_users_own" SELECT only). INSERT via service-role bypassa RLS. Nenhum trigger documented na migration. Testar com 1 INSERT primeiro, verificar antes de seguir. |
> | Daily cap quebra orchestrate-content (que também usa mcoCoins) | Low | Cap filtra `action LIKE 'canvas_%'`. orchestrate-content usa `action='spend'` default. Confirmar nos callers de deduct_mco_coins antes do deploy. |
> | Crontab usa timezone errado (horário desalinhado com BRT) | Medium | Step 0 de Phase 3.7 obriga `timedatectl` check antes do swap. Plan inclui ambas matrizes de horário (BRT-local + UTC-converted). |
> | Sovereign atualizou OE03 com espaçamento 2h e janela 03/05/07 BRT | Resolved | Plan atualizado: Stage 1 03:00, Stage 2 05:00, Stage 3 07:00 BRT. Briefing chega no Telegram alinhado com horário de despertar humano. |
> | News Impact Analyzer custa crédito Firecrawl/OpenRouter sem ROI claro | Low | $0.05/dia × 365 = $18/ano. Aceitável. Pode ser desabilitado removendo do Stage 3 sem quebrar nada. |
> | Re-audit v2 ainda fica abaixo de 4/5 por algum pilar inesperado | Low | Phase 4 explicitamente sobe 3+5; 1/2/4 não regridem. Caso fique, Phase 4.4 documenta o gap e mantém Canvas 4.2c bloqueado — não fabricar score. |
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Sequência de execução proposta
> 
> 1. **Pause for Sovereign approval** (ExitPlanMode).
> 2. Sovereign approves → executar Phase 1 (verificações + Cérebro sem mãos request para JWT bridge).
> 3. Sovereign envia JWT response (ou apenas service-role suffice) → executar Phase 2 (insert reconciliation).
> 4. Confirmar drift=0 → escrever SOP de Phase 3.1 + 4.1 → implementar Phase 3.2-3.6 + 4.2-4.3.
> 5. Smoke test isolado de Stage 1 + tsc + build → confirma OK.
> 6. **Confirmar timezone com Sovereign** (BRT vs UTC) antes de tocar crontab.
> 7. Crontab swap + smoke real de Stages 2 e 3 na próxima madrugada (ou manual trigger imediato).
> 8. Re-audit Phase 4.4 → escrever v2.
> 9. Seal v6.6.0 com 12 commits granulares.
> 
> ```

> **Tool Result (Read)** - ✅ Success
>
> ```
> 1	# SOP — Canvas Job Failure Recovery
> 2	
> 3	**Versão:** v1 · **Selada:** 2026-05-16 · **Lei 2 (Processo Antecipado)**
> 4	
> 5	## ORO triplet
> 6	
> 7	- **Operator:** Sovereign (Gabriel) ou engineer agent autorizado
> 8	- **Reviewer:** Sovereign
> 9	- **Owner:** Sovereign até Phase Commercial; depois Owner do usuário afetado (revenue impact por cliente perdido)
> 10	
> 11	## Contexto
> 12	
> 13	`canvas-execute` cobra credits intencionados antes da inferência. Em sucesso, `deduct_mco_coins` debita; em falha, nada é debitado (atomic on success). Porém o campo `vm_canvas_executions.credits_charged` aparenta cobrança mesmo em falhas — confusão de UI/auditoria.
> 14	
> 15	`higgsfield-webhook` é idempotente (HTTP 409) e só debita após download + validação de bytes (≥100KB + content-type video/*).
> 16	
> 17	## Sequence — execução manual humana
> 18	
> 19	| # | Action | Output esperado | Verification gate |
> 20	|---|--------|-----------------|-------------------|
> 21	| 1 | Identificar a execução falhada via Canvas Studio UI ou SQL | `vm_canvas_executions.id` (UUID) + `error_message` + `provider`/`model` | UUID match em formato 8-4-4-4-12 |
> 22	| 2 | Verificar se houve deduct: query `mcoin_transactions` para o `execution_id` no `context` | Linha negativa OU zero linhas | Se zero linhas E status='failed' → atomic correto; pular step 5 |
> 23	| 3 | Identificar causa raiz no `error_message` | Mensagem do provider (e.g., "OpenAI error 429", "no result_url") | Mensagem não-vazia |
> 24	| 4 | Decidir retry vs descarte: rate limit → wait + retry; auth/key → fix env; conteúdo → ajustar prompt | Decisão registrada | Documentar no JIRA/issue ou comentário no plan file |
> 25	| 5 | (Se houve deduct indevido) Reembolsar via `award_mco_coins`: `npx supabase ...` ou painel Studio | UUID da linha award + balance atualizado | `SELECT mco_balance FROM profiles WHERE id=...` reflete o reembolso |
> 26	| 6 | Re-disparar a execução (novo `node_id` ou retry com mesmo) | Nova `vm_canvas_executions.id` | Status='running' inicialmente |
> 27	| 7 | Acompanhar status até final | status='success' OU 'failed' | `webhook_received_at` preenchido (para video) |
> 28	
> 29	## Verification gates
> 30	
> 31	Cada step só completa quando o gate material é atendido. Não pular gates "porque pareceu OK".
> 32	
> 33	## Recovery path
> 34	
> 35	- **Step 5 falhou (RPC `award_mco_coins` retornou erro):** verificar `p_amount` ≤ 1000 (limit da RPC). Se válido, fallback: UPDATE manual em `profiles.mco_balance` via Studio + INSERT manual em `mcoin_transactions` com action='manual_refund'.
> 36	- **Step 6 segue falhando (3 tentativas):** marcar execução como "abandoned" via UPDATE direto (`status='failed'`, `error_message='abandoned after 3 retries'`). Abrir incident OTD-CM-NNN.
> 37	
> 38	## Success signal
> 39	
> 40	- `vm_canvas_executions.status = 'success'`
> 41	- `output_url` preenchido (signed URL acessível por curl HEAD)
> 42	- Linha negativa correspondente em `mcoin_transactions` (deduct registrado)
> 43	- `infra_health_logs` mostra entry recente com `service='canvas-execute'` ou `'higgsfield-webhook'` e `status='healthy'`
> 44	
> 45	## Anti-patterns
> 46	
> 47	- ❌ "Tentar de novo até funcionar" sem identificar causa
> 48	- ❌ Reembolsar antes de confirmar que houve deduct
> 49	- ❌ Marcar status='success' manualmente sem `output_url` real
> 50	
> 51	## Referências
> 52	
> 53	- `supabase/functions/canvas-execute/index.ts:232-267`
> 54	- `supabase/functions/higgsfield-webhook/index.ts:91-222`
> 55	- `.claude/context/survival-audit-v1.md` (5 execuções falhadas documentadas em 2026-05-14/15)
> 56	
> ```
