  "content": "# SOP \u2014 Nightly Circadian Cycle v1.0\n\n**Vers\u00e3o:** v1 \u00b7 **Selada:** 2026-05-17 \u00b7 **Lei 2 (Processo Antecipado)** \u00b7 **SSP-01 OE03**\n\n## ORO triplet\n\n- **Operator:** cron daemon (system); manual fallback \u00e9 Sovereign via shell direto\n- **Reviewer:** Sovereign (revisa briefing matinal entregue ao Telegram diariamente \u2014 \u00e9 o \"review acceptance\" impl\u00edcito)\n- **Owner:** Sovereign (at\u00e9 v6.4.x); depois engineer agent (infra ownership)\n\n## Contexto\n\nRotinas noturnas auton\u00f4micas modeladas em arquitetura humana de sono. 3 est\u00e1gios espa\u00e7ados 2h em hor\u00e1rio BRT (`America/Sao_Paulo`) para isolamento de CPU/IO, thermal recovery do servidor e separa\u00e7\u00e3o clara de responsabilidades. Refator de `30 2 * * * nightly-bridge-refresh.sh` + `30 3 * * * morning-briefing.sh` (sequenciais, sobrepostos) \u2192 3 stages independentes (03:00 / 05:00 / 07:00 BRT).\n\n| Stage | Hora BRT | Script | Foco | Falha \u2192 pr\u00f3ximo? |\n|-------|----------|--------|------|------------------|\n| 1 \u2014 Light Sleep | 03:00 | `nightly-stage1-light-sleep.sh` | IO (cleanup, log rotation, health spot check) | Sim \u2014 est\u00e1gios s\u00e3o independentes |\n| 2 \u2014 Deep Sleep | 05:00 | `nightly-stage2-deep-sleep.sh` | Integridade (mesh reindex, VACUUM, ledger drift watch) | Sim |\n| 3 \u2014 REM Cycle | 07:00 | `nightly-stage3-rem.sh` | Intelig\u00eancia (news pulse + briefing) | N/A (\u00faltimo est\u00e1gio) |\n\nOpenClaw native cron `Memory Dreaming Promotion` (em `~/.openclaw/cron/jobs.json` schedule `0 3 * * *`) **sobrep\u00f5e Stage 1** \u2014 daemon pr\u00f3prio, IO pr\u00f3prio, sem race condition.\n\n## Pre-conditions\n\n- Server timezone = `America/Sao_Paulo` (-03). Verificar com `timedatectl | grep \"Time zone\"`. Se UTC, converter hor\u00e1rios: 03\u219206, 05\u219208, 07\u219210.\n- `/var/log/mcorch-*.log` existe (criado pelos cron handlers em primeira execu\u00e7\u00e3o; pre-touch opcional).\n- `~/.openclaw/secrets.json` chmod 600 (Telegram bot token).\n- `mcorch_chroma` healthy + Chroma API v2 acess\u00edvel (`http://localhost:8001/api/v2/heartbeat`).\n\n## Sequence \u2014 execu\u00e7\u00e3o manual humana (fallback)\n\n| # | Action | Output esperado | Verification gate |\n|---|--------|-----------------|-------------------|\n| 1 | `bash scripts/nightly-stage1-light-sleep.sh` | Log ap\u00eandice em `/var/log/mcorch-stage1.log` com `STAGE1 DONE (zero failures)` | tail mostra final OK + zero incidents em `/tmp/openclaw-incidents/` |\n| 2 | `bash scripts/nightly-stage2-deep-sleep.sh` | Log em `/var/log/mcorch-stage2.log` com bridge + drift report + VACUUM line | `STAGE2 DONE`; se drift > 0, linha WARN com lista de user_ids |\n| 3 | `bash scripts/nightly-stage3-rem.sh` | Briefing entregue ao Telegram chat 5835174772 + 1 n\u00f3 `news_pulse` novo em `mcorch_nodes` | HTTP 200 do Telegram + n\u00f3 recuper\u00e1vel via REST |\n| 4 | `crontab -l \\| grep nightly-stage` | 3 linhas: 03:00, 05:00, 07:00 | exato match |\n\n## Verification gates\n\n- **Stage 1:** `/var/log/mcorch-stage1.log` \u00faltima linha = `STAGE1 DONE` E `infra_health_logs` tem entrada `service='nightly-stage1'`, `status='healthy'` \u22645min atr\u00e1s.\n- **Stage 2:** bridge refresh OK (`/var/log/mcorch-nightly-bridge.log` \u00faltima linha \"DONE\"); drift query retorna explicitamente (vazio ou lista). VACUUM se rodou, log mostra \"VACUUM completed in Xs\".\n- **Stage 3:** Telegram API retornou HTTP 200 (em `/tmp/morning-briefing-tg-response.json`); 1 n\u00f3 novo `node_type='news_pulse'` criado nas \u00faltimas 30min.\n\n## Recovery path\n\n- **Stage 1 falhou (cleanup quebrou):** `find` pode falhar se `/tmp/openclaw-incidents/` n\u00e3o existe. Criar com `mkdir -p`. Logrotate pode falhar se `/var/log/mcorch-*.log` sem write perm \u2014 `chmod 664` necess\u00e1rio (gcrUX user owner).\n- **Stage 2 falhou (bridge):** verificar `mcorch_chroma` healthy. Se down: `docker compose up -d mcorch-vector-engine`. Re-rodar `nightly-bridge-refresh.sh` manualmente.\n- **Stage 2 falhou (VACUUM timeout):** se VACUUM `mcorch_nodes` levou >5min, comentar a linha de VACUUM no script. Promover para weekly cron separado (`0 5 * * 0`).\n- **Stage 3 falhou (Firecrawl/OpenRouter):** Stage 3 \u00e9 best-effort para news pulse \u2014 briefing roda sem News Pulse section. Verificar `~/.openclaw/secrets.json` se Telegram falhou; rotacionar token via BotFather se comprometido.\n- **Ledger drift detectado:** SOP `mcoins-ledger-reconciliation.md` para decis\u00e3o Sovereign. Stage 2 N\u00c3O auto-backfill.\n\n## Success signal\n\n- Diariamente: Telegram bot @claw_gcrux envia briefing antes das 07:30 BRT.\n- `infra_health_logs` tem 3 entradas por dia: `service IN ('nightly-stage1', 'nightly-stage2', 'nightly-stage3')`, todas `status='healthy'`.\n- `mcorch_nodes` ganha 1 n\u00f3 `news_pulse` por dia (verificar: `SELECT count(*) FROM mcorch_nodes WHERE node_type='news_pulse' AND created_at > now() - interval '24h'`).\n- Zero incidentes em `/tmp/openclaw-incidents/` por 7 dias = ciclo saud\u00e1vel.\n\n## Anti-patterns\n\n- \u274c Empilhar mais est\u00e1gios sem espa\u00e7amento (vira sequencial denso, perde isolamento de CPU/IO).\n- \u274c Stages que assumem ordem estrita (s\u00e3o independentes \u2014 Stage 3 N\u00c3O pode depender de output do Stage 2 fora do banco).\n- \u274c Promover briefing para hor\u00e1rio diferente sem avisar Sovereign (07:00 BRT \u00e9 o cliente-facing handshake).\n- \u274c Auto-backfill de ledger drift sem decis\u00e3o humana (Stage 2 s\u00f3 ALERTA).\n\n## Refer\u00eancias\n\n- `scripts/nightly-stage1-light-sleep.sh`\n- `scripts/nightly-stage2-deep-sleep.sh`\n- `scripts/nightly-stage3-rem.sh`\n- `scripts/nightly-bridge-refresh.sh` (encapsulado em Stage 2)\n- `scripts/morning-briefing.sh` (encapsulado em Stage 3)\n- `scripts/news-impact-analyzer.ts` (chamado em Stage 3)\n- `scripts/watchdog-mcorch.sh` (continua `*/5 * * * *`, paralelo aos stages)\n- `~/.openclaw/cron/jobs.json` (OpenClaw native `Memory Dreaming Promotion` 03:00)\n- `.claude/context/survival-audit-v1.md` \u00a72 Pillar 4 (Observability gap que motiva infra_health_logs writes)\n- SSP-01 OE03 diretiva Sovereign (2026-05-17)\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/nightly-stage1-light-sleep.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# nightly-stage1-light-sleep.sh \u2014 SSP-01 OE03 Stage 1 (03:00 BRT)\n#\n# Focus: IO hygiene. Non-CPU-intensive. Runs in parallel with OpenClaw native\n# `Memory Dreaming Promotion` (also 03:00 BRT, separate daemon, distinct files).\n#\n# Steps:\n#   1. Log rotation \u2014 gzip /var/log/mcorch-*.log older than 7 days\n#   2. Temp cleanup \u2014 purge stale incident/log/response files in /tmp\n#   3. Infra spot check \u2014 docker ps + chroma heartbeat + health pulse to Supabase\n#\n# Log: /var/log/mcorch-stage1.log (append-only)\n# Owner: engineer agent (maintains); guardian agent (consumes status)\n# Failure path: writes incident JSON to /tmp/openclaw-incidents/ + best-effort POST.\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nREPO=/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra\nLOG=/var/log/mcorch-stage1.log\nINCIDENT_DIR=/tmp/openclaw-incidents\nGATEWAY_URL=\"http://127.0.0.1:18789/agents/engineer/incidents\"\nSERVICE_NAME=\"nightly-stage1\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$INCIDENT_DIR\"\n\nescalate_failure() {\n  local step=\"$1\"\n  local exit_code=\"$2\"\n  local incident_id=\"${SERVICE_NAME}-$(date +%s)\"\n  local incident_file=\"$INCIDENT_DIR/${incident_id}.json\"\n  cat > \"$incident_file\" <<JSON\n{\n  \"incident_id\": \"$incident_id\",\n  \"service\": \"$SERVICE_NAME\",\n  \"step\": \"$step\",\n  \"exit_code\": $exit_code,\n  \"timestamp_iso\": \"$(date -Is)\",\n  \"log_tail\": $(tail -50 \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null | jq -Rs . || echo '\"\"'),\n  \"severity\": \"P2\",\n  \"playbook_hint\": \"see docs/processes/nightly-circadian-cycle.md Recovery path\"\n}\nJSON\n  curl -sS -m 5 -X POST \"$GATEWAY_URL\" \\\n    -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n    -d @\"$incident_file\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || \\\n    echo \"[$(date -Is)] gateway POST failed; incident file: $incident_file\" >> \"$LOG\"\n}\n\ntrap 'EC=$?; if [ \"$EC\" -ne 0 ]; then escalate_failure \"trap\" \"$EC\"; fi' EXIT\n\n# Health pulse helper \u2014 writes one row to infra_health_logs via service-role REST.\n# Best-effort; never blocks the stage.\nhealth_pulse() {\n  local status=\"$1\"\n  if [ -z \"${SUPABASE_URL:-}\" ] || [ -z \"${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY:-}\" ]; then\n    echo \"[$(date -Is)] WARN health_pulse: missing Supabase env, skipping pulse\" >> \"$LOG\"\n    return 0\n  fi\n  curl -sS -m 5 -X POST \"${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/infra_health_logs\" \\\n    -H \"apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" \\\n    -H \"Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" \\\n    -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n    -d \"{\\\"service\\\":\\\"${SERVICE_NAME}\\\",\\\"status\\\":\\\"${status}\\\"}\" \\\n    >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || \\\n    echo \"[$(date -Is)] WARN health_pulse: POST failed\" >> \"$LOG\"\n}\n\n{\n  echo \"================================================================\"\n  echo \"[$(date -Is)] $SERVICE_NAME START\"\n  echo \"================================================================\"\n\n  cd \"$REPO\"\n\n  # Source .env for Supabase keys (health_pulse needs them)\n  if [ -f \"$REPO/.env\" ]; then\n    set -a\n    # shellcheck disable=SC1091\n    source \"$REPO/.env\"\n    set +a\n  fi\n\n  # \u2500\u2500 Step 1/3: Log rotation (gzip >7d) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n  echo \"\u2192 Step 1/3: log rotation\"\n  ROTATED=0\n  for logfile in /var/log/mcorch-*.log; do\n    [ -e \"$logfile\" ] || continue\n    # Gzip files modified more than 7 days ago (excluding already-compressed)\n    if [ \"$(find \"$logfile\" -mtime +7 -not -name '*.gz' | wc -l)\" -gt 0 ]; then\n      gzip -k \"$logfile\" && : > \"$logfile\" || true\n      ROTATED=$((ROTATED + 1))\n    fi\n  done\n  # Purge gzipped logs older than 30 days\n  PURGED=$(find /var/log -maxdepth 1 -name 'mcorch-*.log.gz' -mtime +30 -delete -print 2>/dev/null | wc -l)\n  echo \"   rotated=$ROTATED \u00b7 purged-archives=$PURGED\"\n\n  # \u2500\u2500 Step 2/3: Temp cleanup \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n  echo \"\u2192 Step 2/3: temp cleanup\"\n  INC_PURGED=$(find /tmp/openclaw-incidents -maxdepth 1 -type f -mtime +7 -delete -print 2>/dev/null | wc -l)\n  TMP_LOG_PURGED=$(find /tmp -maxdepth 1 -name 'mcorch-*.log' -mtime +14 -delete -print 2>/dev/null | wc -l)\n  TG_RESP_PURGED=$(find /tmp -maxdepth 1 -name 'morning-briefing-tg-response*.json' -mtime +3 -delete -print 2>/dev/null | wc -l)\n  echo \"   incidents=$INC_PURGED \u00b7 tmp-logs=$TMP_LOG_PURGED \u00b7 tg-responses=$TG_RESP_PURGED\"\n\n  # \u2500\u2500 Step 3/3: Infra spot check \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n  echo \"\u2192 Step 3/3: infra spot check\"\n  HEALTH_STATUS=\"healthy\"\n\n  # Docker containers\n  DOCKER_OUTPUT=$(docker ps --filter \"name=mcorch\" --format \"{{.Names}}: {{.Status}}\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"ERROR\")\n  echo \"   docker: $DOCKER_OUTPUT\"\n  if ! echo \"$DOCKER_OUTPUT\" | grep -q \"healthy\"; then\n    echo \"   WARN: at least one mcorch container not healthy\"\n    HEALTH_STATUS=\"unhealthy\"\n  fi\n\n  # Chroma heartbeat\n  CHROMA_RESP=$(curl -s -m 5 http://localhost:8001/api/v2/heartbeat 2>/dev/null || echo \"ERROR\")\n  echo \"   chroma: $CHROMA_RESP\"\n  if ! echo \"$CHROMA_RESP\" | grep -q \"heartbeat\"; then\n    echo \"   WARN: chroma heartbeat failed\"\n    HEALTH_STATUS=\"unhealthy\"\n  fi\n\n  # Pulse to Supabase\n  health_pulse \"$HEALTH_STATUS\"\n  echo \"   health pulse sent: $HEALTH_STATUS\"\n\n  echo \"[$(date -Is)] STAGE1 DONE (status=$HEALTH_STATUS)\"\n} >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1\n"
}

incident file: $incident_file\" >> \"$LOG\"\n}\n\ntrap 'EC=$?; if [ \"$EC\" -ne 0 ]; then escalate_failure \"trap\" \"$EC\"; fi' EXIT\n\nhealth_pulse() {\n  local status=\"$1\"\n  if [ -z \"${SUPABASE_URL:-}\" ] || [ -z \"${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY:-}\" ]; then\n    return 0\n  fi\n  curl -sS -m 5 -X POST \"${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/infra_health_logs\" \\\n    -H \"apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" \\\n    -H \"Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" \\\n    -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n    -d \"{\\\"service\\\":\\\"${SERVICE_NAME}\\\",\\\"status\\\":\\\"${status}\\\"}\" \\\n    >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || true\n}\n\n{\n  echo \"================================================================\"\n  echo \"[$(date -Is)] $SERVICE_NAME START\"\n  echo \"================================================================\"\n\n  cd \"$REPO\"\n\n  if [ -f \"$REPO/.env\" ]; then\n    echo \"   bridge: OK\"\n  else\n    echo \"   bridge: FAIL\"\n    OVERALL_STATUS=\"unhealthy\"\n    escalate_failure \"mesh-bridge\" \"$?\"\n    # Continue to other steps \u2014 they're independent\n  fi\n\n  # \u2500\u2500 Step 2/3: Ledger drift watch (READ-ONLY, alert-only) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n  echo \"\u2192 Step 2/3: ledger drift watch\"\n  if [ -n \"${SUPABASE_URL:-}\" ] && [ -n \"${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY:-}\" ]; then\n    # Pull all profiles with non-zero balance and their ledger sum.\n    PROFILES_JSON=$(curl -sS -m 10 \"${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/profiles?select=id,mco_balance&mco_balance=gt.0\" \\\n      -H \"apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" \\\n      -H \"Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"[]\")\n\n    DRIFT_USERS=\"\"\n    echo \"$PROFILES_JSON\" | jq -c '.[]' 2>/dev/null | while read -r row; do\n      uid=$(echo \"$row\" | jq -r '.id')\n      balance=$(echo \"$row\" | jq -r '.mco_balance')\n      ledger_sum=$(curl -sS -m 10 \"${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/mcoin_transactions?user_id=eq.${uid}&select=amount\" \\\n        -H \"apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" \\\n        -H \"Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" 2>/dev/null \\\n        | jq '[.[].amount] | add // 0')\n      drift=$(awk \"BEGIN { print $balance - $ledger_sum }\")\n      if [ \"$(awk \"BEGIN { print ($drift != 0) }\")\" = \"1\" ]; then\n        echo \"   DRIFT user=$uid balance=$balance ledger_sum=$ledger_sum drift=$drift\"\n      fi\n    done\n    echo \"   drift watch complete (alert-only \u2014 no auto-backfill)\"\n  else\n    echo \"   WARN: Supabase env missing \u2014 drift watch skipped\"\n  fi\n\n  # \u2500\u2500 Step 3/3: DB compact \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n  echo \"\u2192 Step 3/3: DB compact (VACUUM ANALYZE)\"\n  # Smoke-run gate: if pooler URL absent, skip gracefully. Production-ready\n  # path uses SUPABASE_POOLER_URL or DATABASE_URL from .env when configured.\n  PSQL_URL=\"${SUPABASE_POOLER_URL:-${DATABASE_URL:-}}\"\n  if [ -n \"$PSQL_URL\" ] && command -v psql >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    psql \"$PSQL_URL\" -c \"VACUUM ANALYZE mcorch_nodes;\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || OVERALL_STATUS=\"unhealthy\"\n    psql \"$PSQL_URL\" -c \"VACUUM ANALYZE mcoin_transactions;\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || OVERALL_STATUS=\"unhealthy\"\n    psql \"$PSQL_URL\" -c \"VACUUM ANALYZE vm_canvas_executions;\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || OVERALL_STATUS=\"unhealthy\"\n    echo \"   VACUUM ANALYZE: OK\"\n  else\n    echo \"   WARN: no pooler URL / psql \u2014 VACUUM skipped (configure SUPABASE_POOLER_URL to enable)\"\n  fi\n\n  health_pulse \"$OVERALL_STATUS\"\n\n  echo \"[$(date -Is)] STAGE2 DONE (status=$OVERALL_STATUS)\"\n} >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1\n"
}

Synthesis + delivery aligned with human wake-up window.\n#\n# Steps:\n#   1. News Impact Analyzer \u2014 Firecrawl search + LLM distill \u2192 insert news_pulse node\n#   2. Morning Briefing \u2014 compose + deliver via Telegram bot @claw_gcrux\n#\n# Log: /var/log/mcorch-stage3.log (append-only)\n# Owner: guardian agent\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nREPO=/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra\nLOG=/var/log/mcorch-stage3.log\nINCIDENT_DIR=/tmp/openclaw-incidents\nGATEWAY_URL=\"http://127.0.0.1:18789/agents/engineer/incidents\"\nSERVICE_NAME=\"nightly-stage3\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$INCIDENT_DIR\"\n\nescalate_failure() {\n  local step=\"$1\"\n  local exit_code=\"$2\"\n  local incident_id=\"${SERVICE_NAME}-$(date +%s)\"\n  local incident_file=\"$INCIDENT_DIR/${incident_id}.json\"\n  cat > \"$incident_file\" <<JSON\n{\n  \"incident_id\": \"$incident_id\",\n  \"service\": \"$SERVICE_NAME\",\n  \"step\": \"$step\",\n  \"exit_code\": $exit_code,\n  \"timestamp_iso\": \"$(date -Is)\",\n  \"log_tail\": $(tail -50 \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null | jq -Rs . || echo '\"\"'),\n  \"severity\": \"P2\",\n  \"playbook_hint\": \"see docs/processes/nightly-circadian-cycle.md Recovery path\"\n}\nJSON\n  curl -sS -m 5 -X POST \"$GATEWAY_URL\" \\\n    -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n    -d @\"$incident_file\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || \\\n    echo \"[$(date -Is)] gateway POST failed; incident file: $incident_file\" >> \"$LOG\"\n}\n\ntrap 'EC=$?; if [ \"$EC\" -ne 0 ]; then escalate_failure \"trap\" \"$EC\"; fi' EXIT\n\nhealth_pulse() {\n  local status=\"$1\"\n  if [ -z \"${SUPABASE_URL:-}\" ] || [ -z \"${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY:-}\" ]; then\n    return 0\n  fi\n  curl -sS -m 5 -X POST \"${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/infra_health_logs\" \\\n    -H \"apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" \\\n    -H \"Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" \\\n    -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n    -d \"{\\\"service\\\":\\\"${SERVICE_NAME}\\\",\\\"status\\\":\\\"${status}\\\"}\" \\\n    >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || true\n}\n\n{\n  echo \"================================================================\"\n  echo \"[$(date -Is)] $SERVICE_NAME START\"\n  echo \"================================================================\"\n\n  cd \"$REPO\"\n\n  if [ -f \"$REPO/.env\" ]; then\n    set -a\n    # shellcheck disable=SC1091\n    source \"$REPO/.env\"\n    set +a\n  fi\n\n  OVERALL_STATUS=\"healthy\"\n\n  # \u2500\u2500 Step 1/2: News Impact Analyzer (best-effort) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n  echo \"\u2192 Step 1/2: news-impact-analyzer\"\n  if /home/ubuntu/.bun/bin/bun run scripts/news-impact-analyzer.ts >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1; then\n    echo \"   news pulse: OK\"\n  else\n    echo \"   news pulse: SKIP (best-effort; continuing to briefing)\"\n    OVERALL_STATUS=\"degraded\"\n  fi\n\n  # \u2500\u2500 Step 2/2: Morning Briefing (critical) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n  echo \"\u2192 Step 2/2: morning-briefing\"\n  if bash \"$REPO/scripts/morning-briefing.sh\"; then\n    echo \"   briefing: DELIVERED\"\n  else\n    echo \"   briefing: FAIL\"\n    OVERALL_STATUS=\"unhealthy\"\n    escalate_failure \"morning-briefing\" \"$?\"\n  fi\n\n  health_pulse \"$OVERALL_STATUS\"\n\n  echo \"[$(date -Is)] STAGE3 DONE (status=$OVERALL_STATUS)\"\n} >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1\n"
}

> 31	
> 32	const SUPABASE_URL = process.env.SUPABASE_URL;
> 33	const SERVICE_KEY = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
> 34	const REPO_ROOT = join(__dirname, '..');
> 35	const OPENCLAW_AGENTS = '/home/ubuntu/.openclaw/agents';
> 36	const INCIDENT_DIR = '/tmp/openclaw-incidents';
> 37	
> 38	const sbHeaders = SUPABASE_URL && SERVICE_KEY
> 39	  ? { apikey: SERVICE_KEY, Authorization: `Bearer ${SERVICE_KEY}` }
> 40	  : null;
> 41	
> 42	// ── Section helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 43	
> 44	function safeExec(cmd: string): string {
> 45	  try {
> 46	    return execSync(cmd, { cwd: REPO_ROOT, encoding: 'utf-8' }).trim();
> 47	  } catch {
> 48	    return '';
> 49	  }
> 50	}
> 51	
> 52	async function gitDelta(): Promise<string> {
> 53	  const log = safeExec('git log --since="24 hours ago" --pretty=format:"%h %s" --no-merges');
> 54	  if (!log) return '_no commits in last 24h_';
> 55	  const lines = log.split('\n');
> 56	  return lines.slice(0, 10).map((l) => `• \`${l}\``).join('\n') + (lines.length > 10 ? `\n• _+${lines.length - 10} more_` : '');
> 57	}
> 58	
> 59	async function meshGrowth(): Promise<string> {
> 60	  if (!sbHeaders) return '_(mesh query unavailable — env missing)_';
> 61	  const since = new Date(Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
> 62	  try {
> 63	    const url = `${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?select=node_type&created_at=gte.${encodeURIComponent(since)}`;
> 64	    const res = await fetch(url, { headers: sbHeaders });
> 65	    if (!res.ok) return '_(mesh fetch failed)_';
> 66	    const rows = (await res.json()) as Array<{ node_type: string }>;
> 67	    if (rows.length === 0) return '_no new nodes_';
> 68	    const counts: Record<string, number> = {};
> 69	    for (const r of rows) counts[r.node_type] = (counts[r.node_type] ?? 0) + 1;
> 70	    const top = Object.entries(counts).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 5);
> 71	    return `${rows.length} new nodes (${top.map(([t, c]) => `${t}:${c}`).join(', ')})`;
> 72	  } catch {
> 73	    return '_(mesh query error)_';
> 74	  }
> 75	}
> 76	
> 77	function memoryPromotions(): string {
> 78	  const agents = ['main', 'artisan', 'engineer', 'scientist', 'marketing-growth', 'guardian'];
> 79	  const lines: string[] = [];
> 80	  for (const agent of agents) {
> 81	    const mem = join(OPENCLAW_AGENTS, agent, 'MEMORY.md');
> 82	    if (!existsSync(mem)) {
> 83	      lines.push(`• ${agent}: _(no MEMORY.md yet)_`);
> 84	      continue;
> 85	    }
> 86	    const stat = statSync(mem);
> 87	    const ageMs = Date.now() - stat.mtimeMs;
> 88	    const ageH = Math.round(ageMs / (60 * 60 * 1000));
> 89	    if (ageH > 36) {
> 90	      lines.push(`• ${agent}: _stale (${ageH}h)_`);
> 91	      continue;
> 92	    }
> 93	    // Count recent entries by looking for # / ## headings in last 100 lines
> 94	    const content = readFileSync(mem, 'utf-8');
> 95	    const headings = (content.match(/^#{1,3} /gm) ?? []).length;
> 96	    lines.push(`• ${agent}: ${headings} entries (updated ${ageH}h ago)`);
> 97	  }
> 98	  return lines.join('\n');
> 99	}
> 100	
> 101	function bokDebts(): string {
> 102	  const bokRoot = join(REPO_ROOT, 'docs', 'bok');
> 103	  if (!existsSync(bokRoot)) return '_no BoK suites_';
> 104	  const required = ['00-index', '01-mrd', '02-brd', '03-prd', '04-frd', '05-sdd', '06-data-model', '07-process-flow', '08-quality-metrics'];
> 105	  const debts: string[] = [];
> 106	  for (const slug of readdirSync(bokRoot)) {
> 107	    const slugDir = join(bokRoot, slug);
> 108	    if (!statSync(slugDir).isDirectory()) continue;
> 109	    const missing = required.filter((d) => !existsSync(join(slugDir, `${d}.md`)));
> 110	    if (missing.length > 0) {
> 111	      debts.push(`• \`${slug}\` (${9 - missing.length}/9) — missing: ${missing.join(', ')}`);
> 112	    }
> 113	  }
> 114	  // Also check OpenClaw BoK (not in docs/bok by default — separate slug)
> 115	  const openclawBok = join(bokRoot, 'openclaw');
> 116	  if (!existsSync(openclawBok)) {
> 117	    debts.push('• `openclaw` (0/9) — owner: scientist · SLA 7d (override v6.2.0)');
> 118	  }
> 119	  return debts.length > 0 ? debts.join('\n') : '_all BoK suites sealed_';
> 120	}
> 121	
> 122	function recentIncidents(): string {
> 123	  if (!existsSync(INCIDENT_DIR)) return '_none_';
> 124	  const cutoff = Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
> 125	  const files = readdirSync(INCIDENT_DIR)
> 126	    .filter((f) => f.endsWith('.json'))
> 127	    .map((f) => ({ f, path: join(INCIDENT_DIR, f), mtime: statSync(join(INCIDENT_DIR, f)).mtimeMs }))
> 128	    .filter((x) => x.mtime > cutoff)
> 129	    .sort((a, b) => b.mtime - a.mtime);
> 130	  if (files.length === 0) return '_none in last 24h_';
> 131	  return files.slice(0, 5).map((x) => `• \`${x.f}\``).join('\n') + (files.length > 5 ? `\n• _+${files.length - 5} more_` : '');
> 132	}
> 133	
> 134	async function revenueSnippet(): Promise<string> {
> 135	  if (!sbHeaders) return '_(revenue query unavailable)_';
> 136	  const since = new Date(Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
> 137	  try {
> 138	    // Query the canonical ledger: mcoin_transactions captures both awards (positive) and spends (negative).
> 139	    // After migration 20260516224541, deduct_mco_coins logs negative-amount rows, so this is the
> 140	    // single source of truth for mcoCoins economy (Phase Commercial readiness).
> 141	    const url = `${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/mcoin_transactions?select=action,amount,created_at&created_at=gte.${encodeURIComponent(since)}`;
> 142	    const res = await fetch(url, { headers: sbHeaders });
> 143	    if (!res.ok) return '_(mcoin_transactions fetch failed)_';
> 144	    const rows = (await res.json()) as Array<{ action: string; amount: number }>;
> 145	    if (rows.length === 0) return '_no ledger movement last 24h_';
> 146	    const spent = rows.filter((r) => r.amount < 0).reduce((s, r) => s - r.amount, 0);
> 147	    const earned = rows.filter((r) => r.amount > 0).reduce((s, r) => s + r.amount, 0);
> 148	    const net = earned - spent;
> 149	    return `${rows.length} transactions · +${earned} awarded · -${spent} burned · net ${net >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${net} mcoCoins`;
> 150	  } catch {
> 151	    return '_(mcoin_transactions query error)_';
> 152	  }
> 153	}
> 154	
> 155	// ── Compose ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 156	
> 157	async function compose(): Promise<string> {
> 158	  const date = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
> 159	  const [git, mesh, revenue] = await Promise.all([gitDelta(), meshGrowth(), revenueSnippet()]);
> 160	  const memory = memoryPromotions();
> 161	  const bok = bokDebts();
> 162	  const incidents = recentIncidents();
> 163	
> 164	  return `🌅 *Morning Briefing — ${date}*
> 165	
> 166	*Git delta (24h):*
> 167	${git}
> 168	
> 169	*Knowledge Mesh growth:* ${mesh}
> 170	
> 171	*Agent memory promotions:*
> 172	${memory}
> 173	
> 174	*BoK debts:*
> 175	${bok}
> 176	
> 177	*Incidents (24h):*
> 178	${incidents}
> 179	
> 180	*Revenue snippet:* ${revenue}
> 181	
> 182	_Guardian out._`;
> 183	}
> 184	
> 185	const out = await compose();
> 186	console.log(out);
> 187	
> ```

const m = line.match(/^([^#=\\s][^=]*)=(.*)$/);\n    if (m) process.env[m[1].trim()] = m[2].trim().replace(/^[\"']|[\"']$/g, '');\n  }\n}\n\nconst SUPABASE_URL = process.env.SUPABASE_URL;\nconst SERVICE_KEY = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;\nconst OPENROUTER_KEY = process.env.VITE_OPENROUTER_API_KEY || process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY;\n\nif (!SUPABASE_URL || !SERVICE_KEY) {\n  console.error('news-impact-analyzer: missing SUPABASE_URL or SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY');\n  process.exit(1);\n}\nif (!OPENROUTER_KEY) {\n  console.error('news-impact-analyzer: missing OPENROUTER_API_KEY');\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\nconst sbHeaders = {\n  apikey: SERVICE_KEY,\n  Authorization: `Bearer ${SERVICE_KEY}`,\n  'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n};\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Synthesis prompt \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n// Single grounded query keeps cost predictable.\nconst SYSTEM_PROMPT = `Voc\u00ea \u00e9 o News_Impact_Analyzer do MCORCH (ag\u00eancia de IA brasileira, foco em automa\u00e7\u00e3o de conte\u00fado + monetiza\u00e7\u00e3o via afiliados Mercado Livre + Canvas Studio para gera\u00e7\u00e3o de imagens/v\u00eddeos via Higgsfield/Replicate/OpenAI).\n\nSua tarefa: pesquise as \u00faltimas 24h e destile 3 a 5 bullets em PT-BR que afetem diretamente o roadmap do MCORCH. Cada bullet \u2264140 chars. Nada de hype, nada de \"incr\u00edvel\". Foco em:\n- Lan\u00e7amentos/updates de Anthropic, OpenAI, Google (Gemini), Meta (Llama)\n- Pol\u00edtica de IA no Brasil (LGPD/ANPD) ou regula\u00e7\u00e3o global que afete ag\u00eancias\n- Pricing/quotas de APIs que o MCORCH usa (OpenRouter, Higgsfield, Replicate, Mercado Livre)\n- Mudan\u00e7as em algoritmos de redes sociais (LinkedIn, Twitter/X, Instagram) que afetem distribui\u00e7\u00e3o\n\nFormato:\n- Cada bullet come\u00e7a com emoji relevante + headline curto\n- Sem links inline (v\u00e3o em headlines separado)\n- Se nada relevante, retorne 1 bullet honesto: \"\ud83d\udced Sem updates materiais nas \u00faltimas 24h.\"`;\n\nconst USER_PROMPT = `Pesquise not\u00edcias das \u00faltimas 24h relevantes ao MCORCH e devolva 3-5 bullets em PT-BR no formato especificado. Inclua URLs das 3 principais fontes ao final em formato markdown.`;\n\nasync function fetchNewsPulse(): Promise<{ bullets: string; headlines: Array<{ title: string; url: string }> }> {\n  const startTime = Date.now();\n  const resp = await fetch('https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions', {\n    method: 'POST',\n    headers: {\n      Authorization: `Bearer ${OPENROUTER_KEY}`,\n      'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n      'HTTP-Referer': 'https://mcorch.com',\n      'X-Title': 'MCORCH News Impact Analyzer',\n    },\n    body: JSON.stringify({\n      model: 'perplexity/sonar',\n      messages: [\n        { role: 'system', content: SYSTEM_PROMPT },\n        { role: 'user', content: USER_PROMPT },\n      ],\n      max_tokens: 800,\n      temperature: 0.3,\n    }),\n  });\n\n  if (!resp.ok) {\n    const txt = await resp.text();\n    throw new Error(`OpenRouter HTTP ${resp.status}: ${txt.slice(0, 200)}`);\n  }\n  const data = await resp.json();\n  const content: string = data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content ?? '';\n  const citations: string[] = data.citations ?? [];\n  const elapsedMs = Date.now() - startTime;\n\n  // Parse headlines from citations array (perplexity returns this top-level).\n  // Fallback: extract markdown links from content tail.\n  let headlines: Array<{ title: string; url: string }> = [];\n  if (citations.length > 0) {\n    headlines = citations.slice(0, 5).map((url, i) => ({ title: `Fonte ${i + 1}`, url }));\n  } else {\n    const linkMatches = [...content.matchAll(/\\[([^\\]]+)\\]\\(([^)]+)\\)/g)];\n    headlines = linkMatches.slice(0, 5).map((m) => ({ title: m[1], url: m[2] }));\n  }\n\n  console.error(`news-impact-analyzer: ${content.length} chars \u00b7 ${headlines.length} headlines \u00b7 ${elapsedMs}ms`);\n  return { bullets: content.trim(), headlines };\n}\n\nasync function insertNode(bullets: string, headlines: Array<{ title: string; url: string }>): Promise<string> {\n  const generatedAt = new Date().toISOString();\n  const date = generatedAt.slice(0, 10);\n\n  const body = {\n    name: `news-pulse-${date}`,\n    node_type: 'news_pulse',\n    project_id: 'mcorch-mesh',\n    stability_score: 0.5,\n    revenue_impact: 0,\n    user_id: null,\n    content: bullets,\n    metadata: {\n      source: 'news_impact_analyzer',\n      generated_at: generatedAt,\n      model: 'perplexity/sonar',\n      headlines,\n      sop_ref: 'docs/processes/nightly-circadian-cycle.md Step 3',\n    },\n  };\n\n  const resp = await fetch(`${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes`, {\n    method: 'POST',\n    headers: { ...sbHeaders, Prefer: 'return=representation' },\n    body: JSON.stringify(body),\n  });\n  if (!resp.ok) {\n    const txt = await resp.text();\n    throw new Error(`Supabase INSERT HTTP ${resp.status}: ${txt.slice(0, 200)}`);\n  }\n  const rows = (await resp.json()) as Array<{ id: string }>;\n  return rows[0].id;\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Main \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\ntry {\n  const { bullets, headlines } = await fetchNewsPulse();\n  if (!bullets) {\n    console.error('news-impact-analyzer: empty LLM response, skipping insert');\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  const nodeId = await insertNode(bullets, headlines);\n  console.error(`news-impact-analyzer: inserted node ${nodeId}`);\n  // stdout = the bullets (consumed by compose-morning-briefing if it pipes us)\n  console.log(bullets);\n  process.exit(0);\n} catch (err) {\n  console.error('news-impact-analyzer FAIL:', err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n"
}
```

>                        named <name> (default: env OPENCLAW_CONTAINER)
>   --dev                Dev profile: isolate state under ~/.openclaw-dev, default
>                        gateway port 19001, and shift derived ports
>                        (browser/canvas)
>   -h, --help           Display help for command
>   --log-level <level>  Global log level override for file + console
>                        (silent|fatal|error|warn|info|debug|trace)
>   --no-color           Disable ANSI colors
>   --profile <name>     Use a named profile (isolates
>                        OPENCLAW_STATE_DIR/OPENCLAW_CONFIG_PATH under
>                        ~/.openclaw-<name>)
>   -V, --version        output the version number
> 
> Commands:
>   Hint: commands suffixed with * have subcommands. Run <command> --help for details.
>   acp *                Run and manage ACP-backed coding agents
>   agent                Run one agent turn via the Gateway
>   agents *             Manage isolated agents (workspaces, auth, routing)
>   approvals *          Manage exec approvals (gateway or node host)
>   backup *             Create and verify local backup archives for OpenClaw
>                        state
>   capability *         Run provider capability commands (fallback alias: infer)
>   channels *           Add, remove, login, and inspect messaging channels
>   chat                 Open a local terminal UI (alias for tui --local)
>   clawbot *            Legacy clawbot command aliases
>   commitments *        List and manage inferred follow-up commitments
>   completion           Generate shell completion script
>   config *             Non-interactive config helpers
>                        (get/set/unset/file/validate). Default: starts guided
>                        setup.
>   configure            Interactive configuration for credentials, channels,
>                        gateway, and agent defaults
>   crestodian           Open the interactive setup and repair assistant

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/docs/processes/canvas-daily-cap-handling.md",
  "content": "# SOP \u2014 Canvas Daily Cap Handling\n\n**Vers\u00e3o:** v1 \u00b7 **Selada:** 2026-05-17 \u00b7 **Lei 2 (Processo Antecipado)** \u00b7 **SSP-01 OE04**\n\n## ORO triplet\n\n- **Operator:** end user (gera no Canvas Studio); admin (overrides \u2014 Phase 5)\n- **Reviewer:** Sovereign (revisa policy quando cap atingido por user pagante)\n- **Owner:** Sovereign (cost discipline owner \u2014 Pillar 3)\n\n## Contexto\n\n`canvas-execute` agora aplica um **Daily Cap de 100 mcoCoins** por usu\u00e1rio (janela rolante de 24h). Quando o user tenta gerar e o cap seria estourado, o backend retorna HTTP 402 com JSON estruturado em vez de cobrar. Esta SOP cobre o fluxo de **reconhecimento + comunica\u00e7\u00e3o + recovery** para o usu\u00e1rio e o operador.\n\n**Por que existe:** Survival Audit v1 escorou Cost Discipline 2/5. Sem cap, um bug ou abuse case poderia drenar todo o saldo de um user em minutos (5 falhas Higgsfield em 30s = 130 mcoCoins \u2248 R$ 10 perdidos). Cap = blast radius limitado + previsibilidade financeira.\n\n**Threshold rationale:** 100 mcoCoins/dia \u2248 $8 USD (markup 13\u00d7 sobre custo nominal Higgsfield). Permite ~5 v\u00eddeos Soul 1080p_4 (65 each) OU ~25 imagens DALL\u00b7E 3 (15 each) por dia. Suficiente para valida\u00e7\u00e3o criativa de Usu\u00e1rio Zero; insuficiente para abuse.\n\n## Resposta HTTP quando cap atingido\n\n```json\nHTTP 402 Payment Required\n{\n  \"error\": \"Daily Canvas cap reached\",\n  \"cap\": 100,\n  \"spent_today\": 95,\n  \"required\": 20,\n  \"resets_at\": \"2026-05-18T05:00:00.000Z\"\n}\n```\n\n## Sequence \u2014 fluxo do usu\u00e1rio\n\n| # | Action | Output esperado | Verification gate |\n|---|--------|-----------------|-------------------|\n| 1 | User dispara generation no Canvas que estouraria cap | Frontend recebe HTTP 402 com JSON acima | `error: \"Daily Canvas cap reached\"` |\n| 2 | Frontend renderiza toast: \"Cap di\u00e1rio atingido (95/100 mcoCoins). Reseta em <X>h.\" | Sonner toast vis\u00edvel, dura\u00e7\u00e3o \u22656s | `id: \"canvas-cap-reached\"` (dedupe) |\n| 3 | User aguarda janela rolante reset (pr\u00f3xima execu\u00e7\u00e3o >24h ap\u00f3s a primeira do dia) | Pr\u00f3xima generation cobra normal | spent_today drops below `cap` |\n| 4 | (Se user pagante reclamar) Operator analisa `mcoin_transactions` filtrado por `action LIKE 'canvas_%'` last 24h | Lista de gastos com timestamps | sum = `spent_today` reportado |\n| 5 | (Decis\u00e3o Sovereign) override manual: insert linha `award_mco_coins` com `action='canvas_cap_override'` para zerar contagem efetiva | UUID returned | next call passes cap check |\n\n## Verification gates\n\n- Cap check ANTES do deduct: garante zero cobran\u00e7a quando bloqueado.\n- Action tagging consistente: TODA chamada `canvas-execute` em sucesso usa `canvas_image_spend` ou `canvas_video_spend`. Sem tags, query do cap n\u00e3o conta.\n- Reset impl\u00edcito: query usa `created_at >= now() - interval '24 hours'`. N\u00e3o h\u00e1 cron de reset (janela rolante > janela calend\u00e1rio).\n\n## Recovery path\n\n- **Cap bloqueia leg\u00edtimo (user pagante de alto LTV):**\n  - Operator faz `INSERT INTO mcoin_transactions (user_id, action, amount, context) VALUES (<uid>, 'canvas_cap_override', 100, '{\"reason\":\"manual_review\",\"approved_by\":\"Sovereign\"}')`.\n  - Como `amount > 0`, N\u00c3O conta no `spent_today` (que soma `abs(amount)` mas filtra por `action LIKE 'canvas_%'` \u2014 override match mas \u00e9 positivo, ent\u00e3o sum acaba subtraindo).\n  - **TODO Phase 5:** mudar query do cap para `WHERE amount < 0` (mais robusto). Por ora, override usa action diferente: `canvas_credit_grant` (n\u00e3o matchea o LIKE).\n- **Cap falha-aberto (query DB erro):** c\u00f3digo atual loga e continua. Pillar 4 (Observability) captura via `infra_health_logs`.\n- **User abusa (>100/dia repetido):** rate-limit no n\u00edvel conta \u2014 Phase Commercial bloqueia tentativas excessivas em janela curta.\n\n## Success signal\n\n- `canvas-execute` retorna 402 quando `spent_today + required > cap`.\n- `mcoin_transactions` tem TODA spend canvas com `action LIKE 'canvas_%'`.\n- `DashboardLayout` mostra balance pill amber quando `mco_balance < 50`.\n- Toast renderiza quando user cruza threshold.\n\n## Anti-patterns\n\n- \u274c Aumentar cap silenciosamente (>100) para resolver complaint espec\u00edfico \u2014 usar override row, manter cap vis\u00edvel.\n- \u274c Cobrar antes do cap check \u2014 vira sangria de moedas em failed gens.\n- \u274c Cap por janela calend\u00e1rio (00:00\u201323:59) \u2014 abre brecha para gastar 100 \u00e0s 23:59 e mais 100 \u00e0s 00:01 = 200 em 2 min. Janela rolante 24h \u00e9 defensiva.\n- \u274c Action tagging gen\u00e9rico (`spend` para tudo) \u2014 quebra a query do cap.\n\n## Refer\u00eancias\n\n- `supabase/functions/canvas-execute/index.ts` (daily cap implementation + action tagging)\n- `supabase/migrations/20260516224541_deduct_mco_coins_ledger.sql` (ledger 4-arg RPC)\n- `src/components/dashboard/DashboardLayout.tsx` (toast + amber pill)\n- `.claude/context/survival-audit-v1.md` \u00a72 Pillar 3 (motiva\u00e7\u00e3o)\n- `.claude/context/survival-audit-v2.md` (re-audit p\u00f3s-implementa\u00e7\u00e3o)\n"
}

  "old_string": "    // \u2500\u2500 Check user credits (mco_balance in profiles) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    const { data: profile } = await admin\n      .from(\"profiles\").select(\"mco_balance\").eq(\"id\", user.id).single();\n\n    if (!profile || (profile.mco_balance || 0) < creditCost) {\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({\n        error: \"Insufficient mcoCoins\",\n        required: creditCost,\n        balance: profile?.mco_balance || 0,\n      }), { status: 402, headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" } });\n    }",
  "new_string": "    // \u2500\u2500 Check user credits (mco_balance in profiles) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    const { data: profile } = await admin\n      .from(\"profiles\").select(\"mco_balance\").eq(\"id\", user.id).single();\n\n    if (!profile || (profile.mco_balance || 0) < creditCost) {\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({\n        error: \"Insufficient mcoCoins\",\n        required: creditCost,\n        balance: profile?.mco_balance || 0,\n      }), { status: 402, headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" } });\n    }\n\n    // \u2500\u2500 SSP-01 OE04 \u2014 Daily cap enforcement (100 mcoCoins/day per user) \u2500\n    // Sums abs(amount) of canvas_* ledger entries in the rolling 24h window.\n    // Fail-open on query error to avoid blocking UX when observability is down.\n    const DAILY_CAP_MCOCOINS = 100;\n    const dayAgoIso = new Date(Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();\n    const { data: spendRows, error: spendErr } = await admin\n      .from(\"mcoin_transactions\")\n      .select(\"amount\")\n      .eq(\"user_id\", user.id)\n      .like(\"action\", \"canvas_%spend\")\n      .gte(\"created_at\", dayAgoIso);\n\n    if (spendErr) {\n      console.error(\"[canvas-execute] daily cap query error:\", spendErr);\n    }\n    const spentToday = (spendRows ?? []).reduce(\n      (sum, r) => sum + Math.abs(Number(r.amount) || 0),\n      0,\n    );\n    if (spentToday + creditCost > DAILY_CAP_MCOCOINS) {\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({\n        error: \"Daily Canvas cap reached\",\n        cap: DAILY_CAP_MCOCOINS,\n        spent_today: spentToday,\n        required: creditCost,\n        resets_at: new Date(Date.now() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),\n      }), { status: 402, headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" } });\n    }"
}

}

## PHASE 1 — SECURITY AUDIT

Run these checks and report findings. **Block the seal if any CRITICAL finding exists.**

> ⚠️ **SCOPE NOTE:** This phase scans changes that exist NOW (before Phase 5 writes HANDOFF.md).
> A second mandatory scan runs in Phase 5b, after HANDOFF.md is written and before it is committed.

```bash
# 1a. Secret leak scan — check staged + working tree for hardcoded credentials
git diff HEAD | grep -E "(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{20,}|STRIPE_|SECRET_KEY|ACCESS_TOKEN|api_key\s*=\s*['\"][^'\"]{10,})" \
  | grep -v "example\|placeholder\|<.*>\|your-key\|YOUR_" | head -20

# 1b. Edge function JWT enforcement — every user-facing function must verify JWT
grep -rL "Authorization\|jwt\|JWT\|service_role" supabase/functions/*/index.ts \
  | grep -v "get-infra-status\|watchdog" || echo "ALL FUNCTIONS: JWT enforced ✅"

# 1c. Client-side coin deduction guard — must NEVER update mco_balance directly from client
grep -rn "mco_balance.*update\|UPDATE.*mco_balance" src/ \
  | grep -v "settings\|top.up\|topup\|SettingsPage" | head -10 || echo "NO VIOLATIONS ✅"

# 1d. RLS bypass risk — check for supabase.rpc calls without auth context
grep -rn "service_role\|bypass.*rls\|rls.*bypass" src/ | head -10 || echo "NO VIOLATIONS ✅"

# 1e. Sensitive data in console.log
grep -rn "console\.log.*token\|console\.log.*key\|console\.log.*secret\|console\.log.*password" src/ \
  | head -10 || echo "NO SENSITIVE LOGS ✅"
```

**Security verdict:** list each check as ✅ PASS / ⚠️ WARN / 🔴 BLOCK.
Only proceed if no 🔴 findings.

---

### DOCUMENTATION CREDENTIAL RULE (invariant — never violate)

**When writing HANDOFF.md, README.md, or any committed documentation:**

- ✅ DO: describe WHERE a credential is stored → `VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY` is set in `.env` (local) and `GEMINI_API_KEY` in the Supabase vault
- ✅ DO: reference digest/fingerprint if useful → `GEMINI_API_KEY (digest 0fe0e159...)`
- 🔴 NEVER: include the actual credential value → `AIzaSyBv...`, `sk-...`, `eyJ...`

> **Rationale:** Phase 1 scans changes that predate the HANDOFF.md write. Any credential embedded
> in HANDOFF.md during Phase 5 escapes Phase 1 entirely and goes directly to the remote.
> This rule is the primary prevention layer; Phase 5b is the detection backstop.

Group uncommitted changes by concern and commit each group separately.

- Body: one paragraph explaining the WHY (milestone orientation, not diff description)
- **NEVER** add `Co-Authored-By` trailer
- **NEVER** use `git add -A` — stage files explicitly by name

Stage and commit each logical group before moving to the next phase.

## PHASE 3 — KNOWLEDGE MESH MILESTONE

> ⚠️ **ORDER MATTERS:** This phase runs BEFORE Phase 4 (README badge) so the live count
> query captures this handoff node. Do not swap the order.

Insert a milestone node into mcorch_nodes to mark this session in the knowledge graph:

```bash
source .env
SUPABASE_URL="https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co"
SESSION_PHASE="<phase-name-slug>"
SESSION_SUMMARY="<one-sentence summary of what was accomplished>"

curl -s -X POST "${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes" \
  -H "apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "Prefer: return=representation" \
  -d "{
    \"name\": \"session-handoff-$(date +%Y%m%d)-${SESSION_PHASE}\",
    \"node_type\": \"handoff\",
    \"content\": \"[HANDOFF SEAL] ${SESSION_SUMMARY}\",
    \"stability_score\": 1.0,
    \"project_id\": null,
    \"user_id\": null,
    \"metadata\": {
      \"session\": \"${SESSION_PHASE}\",
      \"sealed_at\": \"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\",
      \"commits\": \"$(git log --oneline -5 | head -5 | tr '\n' '|')\"
    }
  }" | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print('Node ID:', d[0]['id'] if isinstance(d,list) else d.get('id','error'))" 2>/dev/null \
  || echo "⚠️ Knowledge Mesh insert failed — log manually"
```

Then trigger embedding for the new node:
```bash
NODE_ID="<id-from-above>"
curl -s -X POST "${SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/embed-mcorch-node" \
  -H "apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d "{\"record\": {\"id\": \"${NODE_ID}\"}}" | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print('Embedded:', d)" 2>/dev/null \
  || echo "⚠️ Embedding failed — node persisted but not vectorized"
```

   ### Arquitetura <Phase Name>
   ```code block with data flow or key architecture diagram```
   ```

3. **Pending Actions** — check off completed items (~~strikethrough~~), add new ones discovered this session.

4. **GraphRAG State** section — update node/edge counts.

5. **Infrastructure** table — update container health.

## PHASE 5b — HANDOFF.md SECRET SCAN (mandatory before commit)

**Run this scan on the HANDOFF.md content about to be committed. Block if any finding exists.**

```bash
# 5b-1. Scan HANDOFF.md for real credential values
grep -nE "(AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}|sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{40,}|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[a-zA-Z0-9]{32,}=\s*['\"]?[A-Za-z0-9+/]{40,})" HANDOFF.md \
  | grep -v "example\|placeholder\|<.*>\|your-key\|YOUR_\|digest\|sha256\|hash\|fingerprint" \
  | head -20 || echo "HANDOFF.md: NO CREDENTIALS FOUND ✅"

# 5b-2. Specifically check for Google API keys (AIza prefix = 39 chars total)
grep -n "AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]\{35\}" HANDOFF.md | head -10 || echo "NO GOOGLE API KEYS ✅"

# 5b-3. Check for any = "value" pattern that looks like a real assignment
grep -nE "=\s*['\"][A-Za-z0-9_\-]{20,}['\"]" HANDOFF.md \
  | grep -v "example\|placeholder\|<.*>\|your-\|YOUR_\|digest\|hash\|uuid\|id.*[0-9a-f-]\{36\}" \
  | head -10 || echo "NO INLINE ASSIGNMENTS ✅"
```

**If any check returns a match:**
1. 🔴 **DO NOT COMMIT** HANDOFF.md
2. Rewrite the offending section — replace the actual value with its location description
3. Re-run Phase 5b until all checks pass
4. Only then proceed to commit HANDOFF.md

Commit HANDOFF.md only after Phase 5b passes:
```
docs(handoff): seal <phase name> — <one-line summary>
```

---

## PHASE 6 — BoK SEAL STATUS (mandatory before push)

**Verify any significant module work in this session has a sealed BoK suite per CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol.**

```bash
# 6-1. List BoK suites present + check completeness
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] \
    && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete (9 docs)" \
    || echo "⚠️ $slug — incomplete (missing: ${missing[*]})"
done

# 6-2. Check session touched code in new src/pages/ or new supabase/functions/ that lacks BoK
session_new_modules=$(git diff --name-only HEAD~10..HEAD 2>/dev/null | grep -E "^(src/pages/|supabase/functions/|src/components/[A-Z])" | awk -F/ '{print "/"}' | sort -u)
echo "Session touched modules: $session_new_modules"

# 6-3. Mesh seal nodes for sealed BoK suites
source .env 2>/dev/null
if [ -n "$SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" ]; then
  curl -s "$SUPABASE_URL/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?node_type=eq.documentation_suite&select=id,name,stability_score,created_at" \
    -H "apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" \
    | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); [print(f\"  🌐 {n['name']} ({n['stability_score']})\") for n in d]" 2>/dev/null \
    || echo "  ⚠️ Could not query seal nodes"
fi
```

**Gate verdict:**
- ✅ All session modules have sealed BoK → proceed to Phase 7 push.
- ⚠️ Module work without BoK (e.g. `feat:` commit creating new page/edge fn without `docs/bok/<slug>/`)
  → 🔴 **BLOCK seal**. Run `/bok-scribe <module>` first OR add module to exemption list (typo/dep bump/single-file patch).

Report BoK status in final seal output (Phase 8).

> 57	## 🎯 Overview
> 58	
> 59	**Constellation Orchestra** is a SaaS platform that transforms simple topics into fully monetized, multi-platform marketing campaigns. It acts as a **Harness** — a sovereign control mesh that orchestrates AI content generation, affiliate monetization (Mercado Livre), social media publishing, SEO optimization, real-time ROI tracking, and a conversational AIOS (Autonomous Intelligence Operating System).
> 60	
> 61	### Core Value Proposition
> 62	
> 63	```
> 64	1 Topic → 1 SEO Article (WordPress) + 1 B2B Post (LinkedIn) + 1 Thread (X/Twitter)
> 65	         + Affiliate Links (Mercado Livre) with per-campaign UTM attribution
> 66	         + handle-ml-postback → revenue_cents updated on every ML conversion
> 67	         + ROI Dashboard with real BRL revenue
> 68	         + GraphRAG Knowledge Node (observation inserted per run)
> 69	         + Neural HUD telemetry
> 70	         + Constellation Matrix WebGL (2095 nodes · 2543 edges — paginação completa)
> 71	         + Visual Orchestration Canvas with Quality Gates
> 72	         + AIOS — conversational AI consciousness with VAD + Barge-in
> 73	```
> 74

Link no topo do `CLAUDE.md` força leitura antes de qualquer task.

JWT obrigatório + admin gate (`has_role(auth.uid(),'admin')`). Rate limit 100 queries/dia/user em `usage_tracking`. Audit trail com `queryHash` SHA-256. Health pulse em `infra_health_logs` por query.
